# GLOBAL.md — project-wide translator notes (assembled by Fable 2026-09-25 from the complete read-through, 422 files). Agents only.
Companion files loaded on demand, not by default: GLOBAL-RELATIONS.md (address matrix digest), GLOBAL-RESOLVES.md (index of later files resolving earlier lines; tl_slice.sh greps it per file), GLOSSARY-CORE.tsv (person/place/org/era/event/title/concept rows). Rendering conventions decided so far are in STYLE.md, which outranks any 'best guess' below.

# PART A — CAST
## 1. Principals

### 古郡なつみ (こごおり なつみ, Natsumi Kogori)
- The project's most-used narrator. First appears 00000024:16:0, already narrating. 16, a 2年生, one year younger than 春花. As-of of her CAST block: 00002478.
- Pronoun: 私 (watashi) in narration and dialogue, no switch anywhere. FREQUENCY is above typical — her narration restates 私 constantly ("私にできることなんて、何もなかった" 00000024:16:58). Do NOT delete the English "I" to smooth prose; the repetition is her texture.
- Speech level baseline: タメ口 to 春花, 五島 and her own mother; plain-casual to 春花's mother (おばさん) despite the age gap; 敬語 (です・ます) to any adult in authority and to strangers. As of 00000538 the 敬語 to 伊勢 is unbroken across a whole file and she never drops it with him. Her 敬語 has been overridden only by grant, three times, always by the same kind of move (an adult forbids it): 新村茅萱 (00000944:8:173), 新村エリカ (00000A30:11:185, again in a different branch 00000D59:11:160, again 000012AD:12:11). She corrects herself mid-cell each time — 「わ……わかりました、／いや、わかったよ！」 -> Q136, Q499.
- Particles: ～よね, ～かな, ～のかな, ～でしょ, ～てよ, ～ってば (affectionate exasperation). ～だろ / ～ぞ NEVER (one deliberate one-cell breach, see the register-break below). Copula だ in narration, だよ／だね in dialogue; です only to strangers and to 伊勢 (from 00000538).
- Tics: 「はあ……」 the sigh (FIXED EN: written-out sigh, "*sigh*" is BANNED — see GLOSSARY); 「あは！」 short laugh; 「ふふ」; 「うん……」 as a stalling yes; trailing ellipsis on almost every hesitation; 「さーねー！」 as a playful dodge (000005AC:11:58). Dialect: none (standard).
- EN correlates: contraction rate MEDIUM ("I'm", "don't", never "gonna"); profanity ceiling ZERO — she does not swear once in 39 files, and the invented crude compounds of her comic register are not an exception; sentence length SHORT to medium, often fragments; formality NEUTRAL-soft; vocabulary plain, no slang, no jargon.
- Narration voice, baseline: past tense, first person, introspective and self-deprecating, short paragraphs, one thought per line, heavy rhetorical self-questioning, frequent ……, apologetic toward 春花, and she narrates her own body a lot (nausea, dizziness, sleepiness).
- Narration modes that are separate registers and must stay audibly separate: CLINICAL-FLAT at the mortuary (00000538:11:78-95 — the horror is that the voice does not rise; add no emotion words EN-side, the break is at 11:120-125); SELF-WATCHING (00000641:11:161-173 — she describes her own grief from outside and calls it 「滑稽」/「バカらしかった」; same mode at 00000FCF:11:85 and 00001295); STORYTELLER (000009FE — ~100 cells of sustained です・ます 怪談 with no 「」, plainer and more formal than her own narration, almost no ellipses, her ordinary narration cutting back in twice) -> Q146; NEAR-DEATH (00000E31, a whole file, simple and childlike: 「私……死んだんだもん」 11:13); DOMESTIC/UNHURRIED (00000E95, 000012AD:12, 00001533 — no case in it at all); TELEPATHIC (0000151C — her transmitted thoughts printed with a 「――」 prefix, simpler and younger than her narration) -> Q618.
- The FOURTH REGISTER, the HARD voice, as of 000004E3:27: when 春花 turns on her she gives orders and demands answers — stacked ～でしょ／～の？, flat imperatives, 「バカにしないで」, 「もう一度言えって言ってるでしょ！」. She slaps 春花 (27:58; the narration says it is the first time she has hit anyone). EN must not make this shrill; it is deliberate and controlled, and it switches off the moment she gets her way. Same family: the knife at a man's throat in unbroken です・ます (00000D29:19:31-19:38) and the shouted-questions narration of 00000F0D:173:26-31.
- The FIFTH REGISTER, the 修羅 voice, as of 00000600:19 and sustained for a whole file at 000006E0/000006F9: body-shaming comedy as gloating theatre — feminine-commanding ～なさいよ／～わ／～かしら, invented crude compounds (「双子の能無しチチタンク」), villain laugh 「ふはははは！」, an invented chant. Trigger is her chest. Comic and controlled, not rage; switches off with no comment. Contains no actual swear word, so the ZERO ceiling holds -> Q077, Q098.
- KATAKANA 片言 gag as of 000005C1:11:92 — flat katakana to annoy 春花 (「ワカラナイ」, 「ボッタクリ」); the text names the device 「片言」. 春花 and 五島 answer in the same register at 00000600:19:34-35, so whatever EN device is chosen must be reusable by three characters -> Q073. The OLD-SOLDIER gag (00000761:8:15-21) stacks small-kana slurring on a war-veteran voice for five cells -> Q104.
- TEXT-MESSAGE register (00000473): softer, more apologetic and MORE formal than her spoken voice — full sentences, no fragments, explicit apologies; her messages use straight double quotes in JP, not 「」 -> Q036. LATER (as of 00000EF6): the IZAKAYA register, a whole professional voice — bright service 丁寧 (「いらっしゃいませー！」) with fast comic sparring on top, no ellipses anywhere in the shift.
- Her 直感 (danger precognition) is the spine of her characterisation and its grammar changes: warnings as flat childish one-cell statements at 4-6 (0000082F, 00000846); a verdict rather than a warning from 0000050E:11:21; a PICTURE rather than a voice from 00000D11:11:119; jammed with noise in another branch (000011CC:8:114); domestic and used as a lie in another (00000916). Trigger stated at 00000656:11:118 and confirmed from outside at 00000F0D:56:22 — cherry plus wind together. She reframes it at 00000641:11:7-12 as possibly HEARING someone rather than sensing. She calls the wind voice 桜の声 (00000F9F:11:222) where others call it 春花's father's voice; nobody corrects anybody. Keep every hedge and supply no noun where the text supplies none (0000095B:11:24-26, 35:122).
- 四月病 (the April illness): reported in the past tense with 「らしい」 in the branch where it is cured (0000092D:11:145), gone from 00000656:11:114, still active fifteen years on in another (0000156A). LATER (as of 0000241B): its cause is given by the only witness — blood in a 桜吹雪 at 新村栄一郎's death, not remembered, and the doctor says that is why it is not quite a trauma.
- AS A CHILD (0000082F, 00000846, 0000085D, 00000874; she is 4 in 祀耀788 and 6 at school entrance): bright, loud, NO ellipses at all — the opposite of her adult hesitation. Her address form for her father moves パパ → お父さん between the two years with no comment. EN must not make the child sound like the narrator she becomes. Earliest of all, 0000200D: 「…………」 as her whole answer, then the question her character is built on — 「大人って……／助けたい人を助けないのはどうして？」 -> Q786.
- Second-frame blocks that are HERS but must not be merged with her: "the imagined 新村春花 (00000EF6)" is her hallucination, not 春花; "the eyeless woman (00000EDF)" shares two catchphrases with a voice the narrator names as 「古郡先輩……？」 and the identity is never asserted in either direction -> Q288; "モトキザクラ様" and "A子 and her family" are figures inside the 怪談 she performs at 000009FE.
- LATER (as of 00000F24:112, 000020D8:8, 00002444): the CONVERTED register, three times in three branches, and it is the hardest thing in the project for a translator. She is taken over and NOTHING in her surface changes — 春花 bare, soft タメ口, 「ふふ」, ～でしょ, the concern about whether people have eaten, all intact and all warm, aimed at killing them. The only tells the text allows are content and physical (no sweat, no breathlessness, an impossible route). EN must not cool the voice, italicise it or make it eerie -> Q396, Q392, Q857.
- Open queries on her: Q001, Q002, Q009, Q016/Q017 (resolved at 00000641:11:26-37 — the red dream figure is her father — but the earlier span must still not gender him), Q021, Q030, Q036, Q037, Q043, Q060, Q073, Q077, Q078, Q087, Q098, Q104, Q122, Q136, Q146, Q168, Q189, Q216, Q217, Q219, Q243, Q285, Q288, Q308, Q317, Q318, Q322, Q324, Q349, Q396, Q427, Q456, Q492, Q497, Q499, Q509 (see 春花), Q512, Q516, Q577-Q579, Q588-Q590, Q596, Q605, Q612, Q618, Q663, Q737, Q759, Q771, Q774, Q782, Q786, Q824, Q857, Q886, Q887, Q897, Q976.

### 新村春花 (にいむら はるか, Haruka Niimura)
- The project's second narrator and 古郡なつみ's lifelong best friend. First appears 00000024:16:11; narrates 00000024:47 and :51. 17, a 3年生 (3年1組, homeroom 谷崎), the OLDER of the two -> Q105. As-of: 00002478.
- Pronoun: 私 (watashi) in BOTH narration and dialogue, despite otherwise masculine-blunt speech ("私の財布を何だと思ってるんだよ"). The mismatch is a deliberate character signal, not an error -> Q026. Never 俺/僕/あたし. She keeps 私 while dying (0000050E:11:169). Frequency about typical.
- Speech level baseline: タメ口, boyish/blunt (俗に言うボーイッシュ口調). No 敬語 to anyone, including adults and a police 警部補 to his face. The rule has exactly three exceptions in the project and all three are visible register shifts: a phone call to an officer she has never met (00000B3F:11:34-67, です・ます throughout, no くそ, no お前 — not politeness, an attempt to be taken seriously about somebody else's parents); a face-to-face apology to 五島桃子 for her own mother (00000E7B:88:192-220); and, LATER, her doctor and 谷崎 (00001A52, 00001A5C). EN must make each shift visible and must not make her sound meek.
- Particles: ～ぞ, ～だろ, ～な, ～のかよ, ～じゃな (goodbye), ～だぜ (from 0000034C/00000366), and ～わ once as rough-emphatic and NOT feminine -> Q026. Copula だ. Her narration alone carries ～や (00000024:47:22), which never appears in her dialogue and does not recur in later narrating files.
- Tics: 「よう！　調子はどうだ？」 her fixed greeting to なつみ; 「あははごめんごめん！」; 「おいおい……」; 「ほれ！」; 「サンキュー！」; 「バカかよ」 (heavy from 0000033D, aimed only at 五島 until 00000550:11:112 where it lands on なつみ and is pure affection). Dialect: none, but the register is deliberately rough/male-coded standard.
- PROFANITY CEILING: くそ and nothing stronger, anywhere, across every branch — and it is tested hard. Permitted at ceiling: くそ, ちくしょう, うっせえ, ふざけんじゃねえよ, and invented compounds built on くそ／野郎 (「このクソガキ」, 「くたばれ、このデブ！」, 「食らえ！　このクソブタ野郎！」, 「このコスプレババア」). All of it is affection or defence; none of it is a stronger word. EN ceiling: "damn", "crap" -> Q225, Q391.
- Other EN correlates: contraction rate HIGH ("gonna", "c'mon", "yeah"); sentence length SHORT, imperative-heavy; formality LOW even to adults and police; vocabulary casual-boyish, heavy on food and enthusiasm words.
- FIVE REGISTERS as of 000005AC and the translator must tell them apart: (1) the everyday boyish voice — bright, imperative, teasing; (2) the FLAT voice (000002FA:11:35-59) — short questions, long pauses, a dead laugh 「はは……／は……」, snaps back to (1) without warning; (3) the 形相／魔女 voice (0000034C:11:156-201, all of 00000366, 000004E3:27:0-57) — louder, sneering, ～んだぞ／言ってみろよ, corrupted laughter written as 「はははははへははははへははは」, self-mythologising 「私、魔女だぜ？」; (4) the CONFESSING voice (000004E3:11:87-121, 000005AC:11:78-88) — quiet, halting, no emphatic particles at all; (5) the DYING voice (0000050E:11:164-180) — broken mid-word across cells, still 私, still 「お父さん」, still able to taunt. Do not smooth the fragmentation. A SIXTH exists at 00000779: she impersonates her grandmother for 70 cells in an old woman's 丁寧 and not one marker of her own voice leaks — it has its own CAST block, "the 大魔女 voice" -> Q109.
- Narration voice, baseline: plainer and flatter than なつみ's, fewer ellipses, more declaratives. She narrates secrets she is actively keeping.
- Narration modes: AUTOBIOGRAPHY (00000682 and used again at 00000F24, 0000156A:8:45-68 and 193:76-113) — twelve years in 224 cells, plain past, one fact per line, almost no ellipses and NO self-pity; she reports her own murders in the same flat declaratives she uses for the weather, and the two places the voice rises are both joy. EN must not add remorse words; the horror is the evenness. PLANNING (00000697:8:94-138, 00000761:8:50-65) — numbered priorities, self-interruption, and an imagined conversation with 五島 used as a thinking tool, set in 「」 with no hedge -> Q095; the same tool runs for 36 unframed cells at 00000F57:11:96-131 and is only revealed as imagination at the end -> Q409, Q355. COMIC-HYSTERIC (000006E0:16:166-187, all of 000006F9) — sports-commentary register aimed at takoyaki, 戦闘開始／封印を解いて／完全勝利, a self-title 「鬼才少女・新村春花」, with her exasperated asides staying in her ordinary voice so the two levels must be audibly different inside one paragraph -> Q098. STORYTELLER (000009CF:11:7-49) — the 00000682 backstory retold aloud as a warm fireside anecdote with the deaths removed; the two tellings must stay audibly different -> Q089. TEXT-MESSAGE (000002FA:19) — short, repeated imperatives, and she keeps 私 and her blunt endings even in text.
- Comic incompetence, fixed: geography (00000595:11:79-87 — she puts the Natural History Museum and the Eiffel Tower in one country, then Chile, then Paris, then the Leaning Tower of Pisa and the Sagrada Familia). Keep the same landmarks and the same wrongness -> Q069. Inverted comic incompetence LATER: she is strong in 国語/地理/日本史 and below average in English and science (00001A5C:8:16).
- Her defining move, repeated in every branch: the answer is about somebody else. 「なつみが妹だろうが、私が誰であろうがどうだっていい！／絶対になつみたちを守る……！」 (00000E19:11:185) recurs almost verbatim at 00000E49:11:132 and 00002473:11:153 -> Q216. She delivers a proverb twice and the text flags the mismatch both times (「事実は小説より奇なり、だ」 00000DD1:11:137 with the next cell calling it 珍しく…文学的; 「人間万事塞翁が馬」 00000EDF:53:12). Keep the mismatch visible.
- AS A CHILD: at 5 (0000082F) she cries over a spilled drink because 「ジュースさん、かわいそう」 — grief aimed at an object, not herself; at 5 in the surviving branch (000008D0, 000008E8) she is the one who ends it with 「お母さんが魔女なんていやだ」; at 6 (0000110D:8:72-142) bright with none of the boyish bluntness yet and チガ姉 already fixed; at 7 (00000A7C) she stammers at exactly one person in the project, 新村サクラ.
- Physical constants across branches: a head scar under short hair she will not explain and has no memory of (0000092D:11:90-105, 00000A7C:11:4, 0000110D:8:98) -> Q153; and ONE GREEN EYE, arriving in several branches with no explanation and never resolved (00000E95:11:31, 00000EF6:36:154, 00001CA0:144:10) -> Q222, Q233, Q702.
- Second-frame blocks that are hers: "the 大魔女 voice (00000779)" (impersonation); "the navigator (000000F2)" — LATER (as of 00001CA0:144:0-3) the menu voice is ASSERTED to be her, 立木三日 an admitted 偽名; the blocks cross-reference and must NOT be merged or split into two people -> Q763, Q682. "the imagined 新村春花 (00000EF6)" is NOT her — it is なつみ's hallucination -> Q318. The REFLEX voice of 00000EF6:36:82-135 is hers physically but has no intent in it at all: 「死ねえええ！　なつみ！」 eight times in one cell on an hourly cycle, 無表情, brain confirmed comatose, explicitly contrasted by the narration with the 形相 voice, which had feeling. EN must strip every trace of intent -> Q335.
- LATER (as of 00000F24, 0000156A, 00002340, 00002444): the CONVERTED / 神使 register — after killing her best friend she is warm, ordinary and apologetic in her own voice, and nothing in the grammar changes. Same rule as なつみ's: do not cool it, do not make it eerie -> Q396, Q667. LATER (as of 00002225:11:137-147): her register softens permanently and the text says so — 五島 calls her 女性らしい and she answers 「さすがに言葉遣いはなかなか変わらないけどな」. Tone changes, diction does not -> Q890, Q026.
- Open queries on her: Q003 (resolved 000004E3:27:117, 000005AC:11:78), Q026, Q030, Q033, Q042, Q061, Q069, Q078, Q083, Q089, Q090, Q095, Q097, Q098, Q105, Q109, Q113, Q144, Q153, Q216, Q217, Q220, Q221, Q222, Q225, Q233, Q246, Q278, Q335, Q355, Q391, Q392, Q396, Q397, Q400, Q409, Q509, Q552, Q556, Q573, Q642, Q667, Q682, Q693, Q702, Q729, Q730, Q763, Q764, Q765, Q778, Q787, Q789, Q820, Q854, Q885, Q890, Q895, Q902, Q933, Q934, Q939, Q940, Q941, Q977, Q981, Q987.

### 五島絵梨奈 (ごとう えりな, Erina Goto)
- The third narrator and the 後輩 of the other two. First appears 000001E3:8:12 as an offscreen shout 「古郡せんぱーい！　新村せんぱーい！」; full name only at 0000020D:11:99. 15, a 1年生, and the most linguistically layered character in the project. As-of: 00002478.
- Pronoun: 私 (watashi) in narration and dialogue; also NAME-AS-PRONOUN — she refers to herself in the third person by surname ("五島は真摯に受け止めます！"), which is comic and deliberate. Frequency typical in dialogue, high in narration.
- Speech level baseline: 敬語 (です・ます) to BOTH 先輩, to 伊勢, to 古郡茜 and to adults, unbroken. Plain-form だ/である in her NARRATION. She uses です・ます even when talking out loud to nobody (00000460:11:20-44, marked 独り言). Her politeness is NOT deference: at four years old (0000085D:11:136-168, 00000874) she already speaks unbroken です・ます to an adult stranger and already reasons in probabilities out loud, so the 敬語 predates the friendship and was never a choice. This closes off any EN reading of it as submissive.
- Exceptions to the 敬語, and they are the map of her: (a) her SISTER 五島桃子 gets none of it, ever — 394 cells of telephone at 00000EDF:33/[37] and ~500 cells at 00000F6F/00000F87 with not one です・ます: タメ口, teasing, insults, and 「私……／お姉ちゃんが好き」. It is not a lapse, it is the one relationship the register was never built for -> Q094. (b) 新村夏菜, a younger girl, gets タメ口 in at least one branch (00000F0D:38:156). (c) the ～っす SLIP, four separate recorded occurrences, each a different cause and none of them rudeness: lechery (000006E0:16:105-115, eleven cells while groping 春花, including the stretched loanword 「ヴォリュゥミィ」), exhaustion (00000C81:19:20), embarrassment under interrogation by a ten-year-old (00000EDF:47:60-74, and the child names it), defeat (000012C5:8:152) -> Q094. (d) the KILLING-URGE register (00000EDF:83:211-240, 89:184-250) drops the 敬語 completely, shortens sentences to three or four words, and reasons in legal vocabulary while crawling after a woman she means to kill.
- The 敬語 AS A WEAPON, and this is the single most important thing about her English: from 0000050E/00000522 onward she delivers flat imperatives to a 先輩 (「今すぐ逃げてください」), accusations to adults (「だから私はあなたを許せません……！」), interrogations of murder suspects (00000DB9:11:18-110, which なつみ's narration calls 「もはや尋問だ」 and 「異常なまでに威圧的」), an order to a 警部補 (00000F0D:56:197-200), and a hostile question to the man she is accusing (0000154E:8:125) — and NOT ONE WORD drops out of です・ます while she does it. The politeness staying up under that pressure IS the effect. EN must find a formal register that can be menacing.
- Particles: ～ですなあ／～でんなあ (mock-old-man); ～ですぜ; ～でゲスよ; ～であります; ～ですよね／～ますよね (her interrogation habit). Copula です in dialogue and 独り言, だ in narration.
- THREE COMIC TIERS, and they switch ON AND OFF BY BRANCH rather than once and for good — this is a hard constraint and Q019 originally got it wrong: (1) the ゲス／旦那／御意／であります／ですなあ period-comedy pastiche (「御意、旦那！」, 「元木高校1年、五島絵梨奈であります！」); (2) the 小生 tier, a written samurai-retainer humble register above it — 小生, 御尊父様, 御一統様, 五臓六腑, 万死に値する失態, スライディング土下座 (00000BD6:11:0-20; 春花 shuts it down by pointing out 小生 is a man's word and 五島 switches instantly into a ～ぜ boyish register) -> Q177; (3) the childish layer — 「ばあー！」, 「がおー！」, 「むきー！」, 「えへへー！」, 「ほい？」, 「おおー！」. An EN solution must be able to go silent for a whole file and come back at full strength.
- The 作戦 gag: a running catchphrase with at least EIGHT recorded forms (作戦開始／成功／完遂／失敗／第2弾／遂行／最終作戦開始／「作戦……／失敗です。」). It dies twice as a beat (0000037D:11:94, 00000522:11:168) and is absent from whole branches. Its RETURN is a signal: at 0000151C:8:45 it is the first sign she is no longer frightened -> Q020, Q057, Q626.
- The ニカッと grin is her NARRATION tag (~14 occurrences) and it is a manufactured object, not an expression. Origin 000002F6:11:29-38: she deliberately failed a test to fit in, her mother was upset, she went back to scoring well, and her mother said 「その笑顔、とてもいいわよ」 — so she trained the grin. It is deployed to deliver lies (0000057B:11:42, dropping the instant the target leaves), it is named as strained (00000E49:11:166), and the failure of it is an event the text marks (0000095B:35:89 「笑顔を作ろうとしても作れない五島を初めて見た」) -> Q058.
- 「天才少女」: she calls the label a complex, not a strength — she performs it in dialogue and resents it in narration. Tracking its use is tracking her state: conceded straight with no joke at 00000AAC:11:117 (「私は天才少女です」, and the others hear it as one and she does not smile), used AGAINST her twice in chunk 22, corrected at 33 because she is no longer a 少女 (「天才少女……じゃなくて、天才ですから！」) -> Q098.
- The 呼び捨て WOUND, her core, and it is answered from six different directions in six branches, each of which must stay distinct in EN: everyone called her 絵梨奈ちゃん; 古郡なつみ was the first person to call her 「五島」 bare and that is why she attached to her (000002F6:11:49-61, long form at 00000617:8:102-109 where she states outright that she hates her own given name, that 絵梨奈 is too cute for her, and that ちゃん felt like a wall). Then: her sister uses 絵梨奈 bare (0000057B:11:89; 00000EDF:37:161-172, where the sister says 「初めてだよ。ずっと『あんた』だけだった」); she OFFERS the given name to 新村エリカ and to nobody else (00000A30:11:176); she accepts 「エリ姉」 from a ten-year-old with no protest (00000AF7:11:137) -> Q171; she DECLINES 新村エリカ's offer as 馴れ馴れしすぎ (000013FD:8:298) and then takes it herself one file later (00001414:8:234) -> Q561; she learns her sister chose the name and thinks 「ちょっと好きになれるかもしれない」 (00000F87:11:254-281) -> Q037.
- EN correlates: contraction rate MEDIUM-HIGH but always polite ("I'm", "that's", plus "sir"/"boss"); profanity ceiling ZERO (one recorded breach, 「くそ！」 and 「ちくしょう！」 at her own worst defeat, 00001C5D:19:9-24, and one self-directed 「ちくしょう！」 at 000013E6:8:122 — the only two times she swears anywhere); sentence length MEDIUM, explanatory, often numbered or listed when she reasons; formality HIGH; vocabulary SPLIT — schoolgirl-bright on top, precise technical vocabulary underneath, and the technical layer rises file by file (客観的, 仮説, 検証, 模倣犯, ノルアドレナリン → 未必の故意, 交感神経, ミスリード → 咀嚼力, 嚥下筋, 噴門, 褐色脂肪細胞 → 菌類, 子実体, 炭酸ガス → アルカロイド, 交換輸血). Keep the two layers separable in EN; do not level them.
- Narration voice: plain past, warmer and more emotional than her dialogue, openly devoted, and she keeps calling them 古郡先輩／新村先輩 inside her own head at every age including 33 -> Q034. The bare 「先輩」 (never for 古郡) clusters where she is frightened (00000F24:64:3, 100:53).
- Narration modes, all of them separable: CONFESSIONAL (000002F6, whole file — second person addressed to nobody, repeated 「ごめんなさい」; same closing device at 00000617:8:251); SHUTDOWN (0000037D:11:88-98 — she stops reasoning, the last four lines flat and childlike; do not make it lyrical); HEARSAY (00000522:11:0-8 — opens 「これは聞いた話ではあるが。」, police-report vocabulary, keep the hedge); RAGE (00000522:11:141-169 — she does not raise her voice; the narration describes the body while the diction stays measured); QUIET-FAILURE (00000731:8:75-93 — she throws the notebook down and makes 春花 finish her conclusion, without dropping one です・ます); ESSAYIST (00000EDF:65:70-107 — thirty-eight cool general cells on money, the coldest thing she does); DREAM-REASONING (00000F6F:11:0-39 — present tense, watching her own reasoning being deleted as she narrates it) -> Q219; FIELDWORK (000013FD:8:0-60 — evidence read off what people are NOT saying).
- The DETECTIVE mode is her main instrument and it is pointed at different targets in different branches: crime (00000B6F, 00001214), food (00000EAF's COMPETITOR register — grams per minute, stomach capacity, opponents read for intent), botany (the 荒田桜 exposition at 00000F24:94:87-141 and 100:177-243, the largest single piece of reasoning in the project), fungus and state cover-ups at 33 (0000156A). Fixed habits: one premise per cell, explicit hedges (「可能性の話です」, 「全部推測ですけどね」, 「我ながら妄想的な推理」), consent checks before each hard step, announced resets (「ここまでの推理はすべてリセットです」), and on-screen self-correction. KEEP EVERY HEDGE: she disclaims her biggest deduction four times and is right every time -> Q381.
- Her CREDO, stated twice in almost identical words (00001C5D:19:46-53 after the method fails; 00002450:12:72-75 to start an investigation): there is no occult, only labels on the not-yet-explained, and 「必ずそこには因果律が存在するはずだ」. Fix one EN wording and reuse it verbatim.
- The TEACHING habit is a fixed device: a real digression (astaxanthin, octopus lifespan, bear intelligence, Portuguese coffee sizes) that turns into the argument four cells later, with 「ちょっと知識をひけらかしただけですよ」 attached -> Q465. The READING-ALOUD register (00000F24:100:77-154, 00001247:8:75-147) performs an old text in です・ます with the quoted speeches in 『』 -> Q385.
- 「Que droga」 is hers, a Portuguese filler (00000BD6:11:15) and the throwaway gag that produces ドローガ when she says it in front of 新村茅萱 -> Q178. THE SPELT-OUT MORA is hers and 古郡茜's — 「ま・／じ・／め！」 (000016AE:11:64-67), 「し・／あ・／わ・／せ。」 (0000191C:11:161-164). One EN solution must serve both speakers -> Q115, Q084.
- WITH HER SISTER she regresses completely: 「おねえちゃーん！」 and crying 「幼稚園児のように」 (0000057B:11:94). No 敬語, no pastiche, no logic. Only this person gets that voice. Same voice in public at 00000F6F:11:193.
- LATER (as of 00000EAF): she is MUTE for a whole 2241-line file and EVERY utterance she makes is written — the engine marks sign language, notepad, email and phone messages with straight double quotes and reserves 「」 for other people. Four mute channels: 手話, notepad, a finger × drawn across the mouth, and the ニカッと grin used as an answer -> Q254, Q269, Q036. Her 敬語 survives in writing with the entire comic apparatus gone.
- Second-frame material: at 00000989:11:33-46 she duels an impression of herself and names herself 「五島エリカ」's original -> Q139; the 怪盗ゴトー troupe (00000EDF) is a game she plays with a ten-year-old; the reconstructed conversation at 00002444:26:306-355 is ~48 cells of dialogue that never happened, framed only by 「きっとこんなやり取りがあったはずだ」 -> Q937.
- Open queries on her: Q015, Q019, Q020, Q030, Q031, Q034, Q036, Q037, Q045, Q053, Q057, Q058, Q060, Q063, Q078, Q084, Q087, Q092, Q094, Q095, Q098, Q100, Q115, Q133, Q139, Q146, Q166, Q171, Q177, Q178, Q185, Q219, Q243, Q245, Q254-Q256, Q259, Q263, Q267, Q269, Q285, Q291, Q381, Q385, Q412, Q415, Q433, Q456, Q464-Q468, Q480, Q493, Q504, Q557-Q559, Q561, Q562, Q615, Q626, Q627, Q645, Q646, Q663, Q688, Q697, Q713, Q721, Q722, Q811, Q823, Q834, Q857, Q862, Q888, Q897, Q900, Q901, Q933, Q937, Q950.

### 古郡良治 (こごおり りょうじ, Ryoji Kogori) — なつみ's father
- Referenced from 000001DB, NAMED on screen at 000004E3:11:1. In the story present of the earliest branch he is already dead, and his living material comes from narration runs set twelve years earlier. As-of: 00002444.
- Pronoun: 俺, at typical frequency. Copula だ. Dialect: none. Profanity ceiling: くそ／ちくしょう, and it holds even while he dies (000008B9:36:67, 000008E8:8:78, 00000CE3:11:43).
- Occupation: engineer at a phone company; he designed the model he gives his daughter (00000538:11:107) and later builds a listening app into a second one and a decoy weight so the phone imitates a bugged phone (00000CE3:11:136-197). The app is a plot device in several branches.
- FIVE REGISTERS and the gap between the first two IS the character: (1) the DAD register — warm, teasing, self-deprecating, short and generous and a little goofy (「はは！　いいのか俺なんかで。」, 「何、大したことじゃないさ。」, 「もしもし、なつみか？」, 「よし、いいぞ」); (2) the LETTER register (00000564:11:97-120) — terse, ordered, unsentimental, one clause of reasoning per line, no softeners, never once says he is afraid, sign-off is the whole of 「頼むぞ。」, with the repeated frame 「できるだけ、普段通りに」 — normality as an instruction; (3) the VOICELESS register (00000641:11:32-60) — skinned and unable to form consonants; his daughter's name takes three cells (「な……／つ……／み……」), breath escapes as 「ヒィ、ヒィ」, and the OBJECT LANDS LAST in both of his messages. Warm, not frightening: the narration says the touch feels like wind and there is no revulsion -> Q085. The same sound arrives as 「あ……がが……」 in the branch that produces the body (00000CFA:11:337); (4) the REASONING-IN-STEPS mode, which is his narration voice; (5) the FATHER register (0000085D, whole file) — fussing and comic, out-argued about probability by two small girls, lining up ten glasses of water. It is the same dry voice on a trivial subject, not a softer one.
- Particles: ～からな／～ぞ／～さ (dad); ～てほしい／～な (prohibitive)／～てくれ (letter). Tics: 「はは！」; 「何、～さ」 as a brush-off.
- EN correlates: contraction rate MEDIUM in speech, LOW to NONE in the letter; profanity ceiling くそ; sentence length SHORT in both; formality LOW then MEDIUM-formal; vocabulary ordinary salaryman warmth, then careful legal-ish caution (証拠, 公になれば, 周知).
- HE NARRATES a nine-file run (0000082F through 000008E8, orders 66-74), twelve years before the story present, and it is the FIRST MALE 俺 NARRATION in the project. A translator working file by file must not assume 古郡なつみ or 新村美冬 is narrating any of them. LATER he also narrates 00000CB3 (order 113), 00000CE3 (order 115) and 00001AC1 (order 258) -> Q030, Q078.
- Narration voice: plain past, 俺, short declaratives, dry and unsentimental. He jokes about money and about being out-ranked at home, explains his job in concrete nouns, and reasons in numbered steps when frightened. Two things break the flatness and BOTH ARE REFUSALS: at 0000082F:11:154-156 he stops narrating and addresses the falling man in the second person without finishing the sentence, and at 11:157-161 he describes only holding the child. He never says what he saw — EN must not supply it.
- The REASONING mode at its fullest (000008B9:29:29-60, 36:49-69): numbered character traits set in 『』 — 『バカではない』『極端』『根は甘えん坊』『純朴で素直』 — used as a working model of another person, then a list of what still does not fit, ending 「くそ……！　どうしても推理が追いつかない……」. He is right about the person and wrong about the danger. Do not let EN make him sound clever or foolish; he is neither.
- The TIME-SLOWS mode (000008D0:27:0-24): twenty-four cells of one-sentence self-interrogation while a mace falls, each cell a question, none answered, ending on 「俺は……／俺は……！」. No physical description at all. Keep the questions and the repetition.
- Address forms are FIXED from 788 to 800 with nothing changing: 茜 bare, お前 for both wife and junior, 栄一郎 bare, 美冬さん with the さん kept even while he fears her, 春花ちゃん. Nothing about him changes across twelve years except that he stops narrating.
- His MORAL POSITION is stated and then retracted, and the retraction is the point. 00000CB3:11:46-51 — 「そもそもこれは、なつみの風船が原因。／その責任は親の俺たちにある。」, 「命を失ったことで被った不幸は、命を以って取り返す……。」, and 「いや、俺はそもそも誰に許しを請うというのだ？／神様か？　警察か？　被害者の親か？」 — then the closing four cells: 「そう、これはある意味正当防衛とも言える。／そうでも考えないと、どうにかなってしまいそうだ。／いや……／もう、どうにかなってしまっているのだろうな……。」 Do not let EN resolve it in either direction.
- His dying lines are always imperatives about other people and he never begs (the text notes the not-begging twice): 「家族には……手を出さないでくれ」 (00000CFA:11:303) then a broken 「な……／なつみ……／は……」 that never reaches a verb; 「なつみ！　早くそこから逃げろ！」 (00000B27:11:111) then blade noise. Do not give him a final sentence he does not have.
- LATER (as of 00000F40/00000F57): his longest comic run — the DAD register at full volume with alcohol on it, walking naked into his daughters' bath, genuinely hurt when thrown out. He still bathes with his eighteen-year-old daughter and neither of them finds it remarkable; 春花 does, loudly, and the file lets both readings stand. 春花's 妄想 of him being arrested for it (00000F57:11:100-108) gets his self-deprecating register exactly right -> Q409.
- LATER (as of 00001477, 00001C02, 00002444): his lines go deliberately EMPTY and the emptiness is the information. 00001477:8:100 is a sentence with the subject, predicate and modifier removed on purpose, and the choice of a message over a call is itself a signal about who is listening — his daughter decodes both -> Q577. At 00001C02 he is the one who stops the trip to 荒田 by refusing to substitute the adults' judgement for his daughter's; 00001BAE is the same hours with the opposite outcome and nothing marks either as true -> Q798, Q054.
- Open queries on him: Q030, Q054, Q071, Q078, Q085, Q328, Q409, Q577, Q646, Q747, Q748, Q798, Q892. Never gloss the letter's 「例の件」 (00000564:11:103) or 「美冬さんの正体」 (11:114); the 00000641 appearance is never labelled ghost, vision or hallucination and both halves of なつみ's 「これが気のせいなのは分かっている」 plus the elbow that actually stops hurting must be kept.

### 古郡茜 (こごおり あかね, Akane Kogori) — なつみ's mother
- First appears 000001DB:15:79. Late 30s / early 40s, a working mother — do NOT render her as elderly. She is 新村美冬's friend of forty years and the two women's history drives most of the plot. As-of: 00002444.
- Pronoun: 私, LESS often than typical — she drops subjects heavily. Copula だ／よ, feminine. Dialect: none.
- Speech level baseline: タメ口, maternal-feminine. Particles ～わよ／～わね, ～なさい, ～でしょ, ～のよ, ～なさいな. Tics: 「ほらほら」; 「ごめんごめん！」; a chirpy apology-then-order pattern; 「あんたねえ……」 aimed at 美冬 across twenty years.
- EN correlates: contraction rate MEDIUM; profanity ceiling ZERO; sentence length SHORT; formality warm-informal; vocabulary ordinary domestic, and she handles police-procedure and prison vocabulary without difficulty when she has to.
- ADDRESS FORMS ARE HER FINGERPRINT and they do not move in any branch across twenty years: 「良治君」 for her husband — the only spousal address form in the project — bare 「美冬」 with no suffix for her friend, 「春花ちゃん」 for the daughter she gave away (including in the frame where she is naming her as her own child), 「五島ちゃん」, 「なつみ」 bare. At 00000641:11:122-124 she corrects 「良治君」 into 「お父さん」 mid-line for her daughter's benefit.
- SECOND REGISTER, the CONFESSION voice (00000522:11:112-139): every chirp gone, long pauses written as sighs, slow plain admissions with no ～わよ energy. She still says ～ね and ～の, so it is the same woman; what leaves is the volume.
- THIRD REGISTER (00000641:11:116-154): in that branch she is the figure in 能面 and 赤装束, and she is dying when she speaks. Short, broken across cells by pain, and entirely about other people. She still uses ～の and ～わ. She never explains anything. While masked she says NOTHING at all, does not react to being called 死神 or 新村美冬, and takes several rounds through ordinary cloth with no vest — do not give her a sound before the mask comes off.
- YOUNG REGISTER, extensive (000007BC, 000007D3, 000007EA, 00000801, 00000818; she is 7, then a high-schooler, then 21, then a wife; and at 15 in 00001A97): cool-elder-sister タメ口 already built on ～なさい／～でしょ／～わよ／「あんたねえ……」. The maternal ～なさい of 000001DB is already there twenty years early, aimed at a friend.
- THE LAUGH, her single most identifying feature in the flashbacks and absent from every present-day file: a suppressed snort written 「ブフッ」, extended as 「ブフフ」「ブフホッ」「ブフフフホッ」. The narration says twice she is a クールキャラ who only breaks in front of 美冬. EN must fix ONE syllable and lengthen it the same way each time.
- THE SPELT-OUT IMPERATIVE, her verbal signature across twenty years and shared with 五島絵梨奈: 「ちゃ・／ん・／と、／ご検討ください！／以上！」 (000007BC:8:311-315, almost verbatim at 00000818:11:235-239 and 000008E8:8:261) and 「と・／つ・／に・ゅ・／う！」 (000007EA:11:13-16). ONE EN device must serve all of these and 五島's -> Q115.
- WHAT SHE DOES, NOT WHAT SHE SAYS, is the character, and it is one register applied at three moral distances: she postpones her own wedding by a year for her friend (00000801:8:31-35); she comes to that friend's bedroom with severe morning sickness to offer her unborn child (00000818:11:59-76); and she asks for the time, place and method of a murder in a voice the narration calls free of hesitation (000008A2:8:15-16). Call this the FLAT COMPETENCE and note that it is her ORDINARY maternal ～わ／～なさい／～の register applied to the task: at 00000CB3 she reads the instruction sheet, tests the drug at the specified half dose, scouts the site, comes back 「何食わぬ顔で」, reports the effects like a technician and assigns the targets — not one hedge and not one question in the file. EN must NOT make her sound cold-blooded; the horror is that nothing in the voice changes.
- Her one long speech (000008E8:8:208-217) diagnoses 美冬 in four adjectives and then refuses to accept a refusal: 「あんたの敵は誰1人いないの」, 「元木町に帰ろう？」, 「もう絶対に美冬を見捨てないから」. No softeners, no questions, four imperatives — the same register as 「おとなしく座ってなさい」.
- The FLAT COMPETENCE FAILS EXACTLY ONCE, LATER (as of 00002444:26:181-199): she slaps 五島絵梨奈 for saying the girl in front of her is not her daughter, keeps the maternal ～の／～でしょ register through 「黙れ！」 and 「なつみは私の娘。邪魔しないで」, and will not meet 五島's eyes afterwards. The file lets her be simply wrong rather than a fool -> Q938.
- She is UNREMARKABLE ON PURPOSE in the branches where nothing happened (00000916:31:36-53, 00000E61:11:6 and 11:74, 000009E6 frame): three lines, a gift for the hosts, 「じゃあ美冬、くれぐれも、安全運転でね」. Keep her flat there; the weight is elsewhere.
- LATER (as of 0000156A): SHE NARRATES A WHOLE 3,299-LINE FILE, her first narration anywhere, and this CAST block carries no narration-voice line before it -> Q640. Narration voice: plain past, 私, short declaratives with the subject dropped, almost no similes and no ellipsis-hesitation — her maternal register turned inward, practical, sequential and self-accusing with NO grief vocabulary. She reports her own body (a sore back, cold feet, her age) the way なつみ's narration does and nothing remarks on the resemblance. Exactly three borrowed images break the flatness, all in that one file: 年老いた浦島太郎, a zombie-apocalypse film with a running commentary on what the genre would have given her, and マッチ売りの少女 -> Q659. Her name appears exactly ONCE in the file, at 92:0, in a sentencing formula, so her third-person address forms are the only way a reader identifies the narrator.
- LATER (as of 0000241B, 00002420): she narrates two more consecutive files in 私 — domestic and procedural, built out of household actions (chopping vegetables, the microwave, the three-minute bath-cleaning limit) with the crisis pushed into the gaps. She reports her own lies as facts about herself: 「自分でも驚くほど滑らかに嘘が出た。／決して気分のいいものではない」. She CANNOT CUT MEAT (11:19-34: puts the knife down twice, escalates through FOUR spellings of the same adverb across four cells, and cooks burdock and dried fish instead) -> Q903.
- LATER (as of 00001477:8:105-125): she delivers the chunk's central hint in her ordinary maternal タメ口 with the content deliberately removed — three sentences whose 「でも」 is a 逆接 contradicting nothing, which IS the clue — and hangs up the instant her daughter names it, closing with 「なつみ、大丈夫、みんなを信じなさい」. The abruptness is protection; EN must not soften it -> Q577, Q578. That line is quoted back twice by her daughter's narration.
- Recorded unresolved gaps that EN must NOT close: she is visibly hiding something at 000001DB:15:155-159; the ORDER of her questions at 00000522:11:91-92 (春花ちゃん first, then 美冬) is the clue 五島 works from — do not reorder; what she admits at 11:123 is never stated; her sentence about 美冬 at 00000641:11:138-140 never reaches its ending; 五島 later asks whether the body returned was even hers (00000656:11:143). The adoption is hers and begins at 00002001 with HER OWN unborn daughter under discussion, not 春花, and the project never states how it was inverted -> Q782, Q216.
- Open queries on her: Q030, Q054, Q063, Q078, Q114, Q115, Q216, Q640, Q646, Q659, Q663, Q667, Q693, Q712, Q782, Q783, Q798, Q892, Q903, Q938.

### 新村美冬 (にいむら みふゆ, Mifuyu Niimura) — 春花's mother
- First appears 000001DD:11:74, addressed only as おばさん. NAME RULE, hard: the name is CONFIRMED in the text at 0000037D:11:58 (written on a 紙人形); before that line she is おばさん / 春花のお母さん and EN MUST NOT use "Mifuyu". From 00000564:11:182 the police say it aloud. In her own narration run (orders 61-65) she is 金井美冬 and the surname 新村 must not appear before 00000801:8:120 -> Q110. Over 40. As-of: 00002444.
- Pronoun: 私, typical frequency. Copula よ／わ, feminine, elongated. Dialect: none. Profanity ceiling ZERO.
- She has SIX documented states of one voice and getting them apart is the single largest voice job in the project -> Q106, Q114:
  - REGISTER ZERO, the original (orders 61-65, 00001A97 at ~15, 00002001, and under duress at 00000F24:187 and 0000156A:193): whining, dependent, NO elongated ー anywhere, and the 口癖 「うぎゅ……」 about forty times. です・ます to 栄一郎 from the first meeting and never dropped, not even after marriage. This is her natural voice.
  - REGISTER 1, the loud one (000001DD onward): タメ口, extremely high energy, a drawn-out ー！ on nearly every sentence (「そうなのよー！」) — her single most identifying feature — plus 「ほほほ」／「おーっほっほっほ！」, 「あらー！」, 「あららなつみちゃん！」, runaway run-on sentences. It is NOT natural: it is an over-correction 茜 built on top of her after the 女ケ沢 crisis, and the acquisition is shown on screen at 00001AC1:8:1-39, where 茜 twice tells her it sounds おばさん臭い and she answers 「前みたいに辛気臭いよりはいいでしょー！」. The text says she tries to sound young and lands おばさん臭い. EN: a 40-year-old over-performing youth — exclamation marks everywhere, elongated vowels ("soooo", "reeeally") as the equivalent of ー, in register 1 ONLY.
  - REGISTER 2, the flat one: every drawn-out ー gone, every exclamation mark gone, short level feminine plain form. Presented as a REVEAL at 00000564:11:179-191 (「能面以上に無表情」) — the absence of her usual noise IS the reveal — but it is already simply how she sounds after the accident twelve years earlier (000008A2), and it runs for four consecutive files with nothing being revealed (00001442, 000014A5, 000014BC, 000014D7). Do not treat every occurrence as a mask.
  - the QUIET-WARM state (00000E61:11:87-103, 00000F57:11:166-171): markers off for TENDERNESS, not for a reveal — 「いい子に育ってくれて……ありがとね。／私と栄一郎さんの世界一の娘だわ」 — and at 00000E61 they all come back inside two cells with 「おーっほっほっほっほ！」.
  - the STORYTELLER state (000009E6:11:8-19:5): stretched vowels and exclamation marks dropped for sixty cells to tell a ghost story levelly, then all put back for the frame. Stagecraft, not the 00000564 reveal.
  - REGISTER 1 AS A TOOL: used deliberately to talk なつみ out of her guilt (0000092D:11:150-157, 00000944:8:111-143), to drive an escape and hold a hole in the ground with a nata (00000E01), and to run an interrogation where the volume stays UP while the content turns (0000113D:8:64-67, 00001184:16:7-85 — 「叔母さんの口調はとても軽い。／だけど私にとっては、とても重い」). EN must not lower the volume for those lines.
- The EARLY BLEED is critical for chunk-level translators: at 00000697:8:195-201 three cells inside the loud register have every marker gone — 「ねえ……春花……／今日はなつみちゃんと……／仲良くね」 — with no expression in eyes or voice. 00000682:11:59 and 00000697:8:202 both say she goes expressionless whenever なつみ is mentioned. Strip the elongation and exclamation marks for exactly those cells and nowhere else in the file -> Q106.
- EN correlates: contraction rate HIGH (register 1) / MEDIUM (register 2); profanity ceiling ZERO; sentence length LONG when excited, VERY SHORT when flat; formality LOW except to 栄一郎, 新村サクラ (サクラさん + です・ます from the first meeting, never changing), her mother-in-law (お義母さん, です・ます, 「は……／はい……」 to an order) and to teachers.
- THE TRAIT THE TEXT KEEPS NAMING IS 極端 (000007BC:8:151-155, 00000801:8:4, 00000818:11:49-50): she goes all the way in whichever direction she is pointed. It is a joke four times before it stops being one. THE LOOK is her other fixed behaviour: 「怖いくらいの目つき」 that sees through a lie, attested in the 00000682 flashbacks and again at 00000FB7:11:105-129. Her question is always the same — 「本当に……本当に楽しいの？」 — and she NEVER asks a direct question.
- She is trained: one year of 山籠 inside 荒田集落 before marrying in, hunting without firearms, 鉈 and メイス technique. She splits a fly in mid-air in a dark room and then says flatly it is within ordinary human training (000008A2:8:71-76). 00000CFA, 000013CD:8:47-143 and 00001442 all run this competence at length.
- THE KILLING REGISTER (000008D0:33, and at full length as narrator of 00000CFA): she keeps her ordinary feminine ～わ／～かしら／～のよ and EVERY address form intact while she kills — 「あら、肺が破裂しちゃったかしら？」, 「ねえ良治さん、聞こえる？」, 「ふう、さようなら、古郡良治さん」 — with only her usual laugh, no shouting, no coarsening, and she apologises to nobody. Register 1's grammar at register 2's temperature. EN must let her be competent; the horror is that nothing in the grammar changes -> Q130.
- The OPERATOR / PLANNER mode (000008D0:46:30-99, 00000CFA:11:95-205, 00002427 whole file): she explains or executes an operation calmly and in order, with contingencies, and her sentences get LONGER and better organised the further she goes. EN must let her be genuinely intelligent here; nothing else in the project shows her this way.
- Her motive is named plainly and never varies across every branch: 「春花と離れるくらいなら死んだ方がマシよ！」, 「私は何よりも春花が1番大事」. Its cruellest use is 00001442:11:67-100, where she drives her own daughter away with 「春花は足手まといよ」 as a deliberate tool -> Q572.
- Three CRACKS in 00000CFA, each one or two cells, and they are the only interiority she gets: 11:211 「さあ、やれよ。やれよ、美冬……。」 — the only second-person self-address in the project; 11:268-274, where she notices her own argument is circular, says 「やっぱり論理破綻してるわー！」 and ends 「あーあ、／楽し。」; and 11:363 「私の心は栄一郎さんが死んだときに壊れてしまったのだ」, stated once and never returned to -> Q206, Q207.
- Narration: she narrates orders 61-65 (five consecutive files covering twenty years), an unlabelled 94-cell inset inside her daughter's file (00000B57:11:51-144, holding REGISTER ZERO and REGISTER 2 at once), 00000CFA (397 cells, her first in the story present — nothing in the first twelve cells identifies her), 00001D40 and 00001D6F, and 00002427 -> Q030, Q078, Q198, Q767. Narration voice: plain past, 私, self-deprecating and almost entirely about other people; short sentences, heavy ellipses, and a habit of comparing herself unfavourably with 茜 in nearly every scene. It is the CLOSEST voice in the project to 古郡なつみ's narration, which matters because なつみ is the one person she cannot bear.
- Second-frame block that is hers: "白般若 (00000F24)" — she is the masked figure, dead two years, SILENT for roughly five hundred cells while her own daughter taunts and kicks her. It has its own CAST block and the EN of the fight scenes must not hint. Her unmasked lines there are six cells of REGISTER 2 at its extremity, she has no pulse and is cold while speaking, and the file never says what she is -> Q388, Q389.
- Deliberate blanks EN must not fill: 「もう……そういう問題じゃないのよ」 (what problem?) and 「私、騙されてたのね」 (by whom?) at 00000564; her last line of chunk 12 (00000B3F:11:121-124) never reaches a verb; the 側-by-side adoption statement at 00000FB7:11:91-94 is cut off by her daughter -> Q216; in the 00000641 branch she has NO lines and the police theory about her is delivered entirely as hearsay, so keep every ～らしい and ～そうだ; LATER (as of 00002444) she is the missing person the branch ends on and the closing cell does not name her -> Q941.
- One-off facts with fixed renderings: she had a stillborn daughter by 栄一郎, five months, buried in the 墓地広場, named ONCE in hiragana at 00000FB7:11:84 as あやか -> Q430. She prays at that grave bare-faced and the narration flags it as against practice. She attended 古郡なつみ's birth (00001184:16:42). She breaks twelve years of 敬語 to her dead husband in three cells at 000014D7:8:58-60 (あんた, a 命令形) and it is the only time.
- Open queries on her: Q030, Q043, Q078, Q106, Q110, Q114, Q130, Q198, Q206, Q207, Q213, Q216, Q388, Q389, Q430, Q458, Q572, Q640, Q693, Q739, Q740, Q750, Q767, Q784, Q802, Q813, Q907, Q910, Q912, Q924, Q941.

### 五島桃子 (ごとう ももこ, Momoko Goto) — 絵梨奈's elder sister, 20 (24 in one later branch)
- First appears 000002F8:12:2 as お姉ちゃん; named at 000002F8:12:17; first speaks 0000034C:11:14 (「あんたか」). As-of: 00002450.
- Pronoun: 私, typical frequency. Copula だ. Dialect: none. Background: dropped out in her first year of high school, dyes her hair, out most nights, resented the 天才少女 fuss around her sister. She waits tables at プリマベラ, an expensive coffee house, and is its 看板娘.
- FOUR registers, switched instantly and on purpose: (1) TO HER SISTER — blunt, clipped タメ口 bordering on hostile, flat statements ending in ～し, deflection instead of answers, and 「あんた」 every time; (2) TO A GUEST — full 敬語 in a bright hostess voice, switched inside one cell, and 五島 notices the switch; (3) THE PROFESSIONAL REGISTER (000005C1:11:26-30) — わたくし, ～でございます, ～いたします, ～くださいませ, 「五島様」 to her own sister, 「お三方」 for a table of three: flawless, warm and completely impersonal. It is the SAME surface form the menu navigator uses (000000F2) and must NOT sound identical in EN -> Q075; (4) the SECOND-PERSON ESSAY register, see below.
- The professional register is a COSTUME and the text proves it three times: she drops to blunt タメ口 for two lines and the narration calls it 「一気に化けの皮がはがれたかのような素の表情」 (000005C1:11:303-308); she comps a bill without being asked and hides the favour inside the polite voice; and LATER (as of 00001807:8:42-63) she knocks a man down, switches face and voice inside one cell into 「お久しゅうございます、道畑様」, and comments 「自称ファンのくせに、表情と声変えただけで分かんないって……」 -> Q707.
- THE PATTERN THAT IS THE CHARACTER, recorded ELEVEN times across the project and flagged every time: ROUGH WORDS, PROTECTIVE ERRAND. 「あんた誰とも電話しないでよ」 is a warning; 「ったく……いつまでたってもガキなんだから……」 is said while her sister sobs on her at a midnight police station; 「そういう時はごめんじゃなくてありがとうでしょ？」 is a lesson; 「行かないでって言ってるの！」 is fear. EN MUST NOT WARM THE WORDING — the warmth is in the fact that she came.
- EN correlates: contraction rate HIGH; profanity ceiling ZERO, with exactly TWO recorded breaks, both triggered by her sister and not by herself — 「今度絵梨奈の悪口言ったら……／マジで殺す」 (00001807:8:62) and 「こんなやつ1人殺せない警察こそとっとと出ていけ！」 (0000242E:11:116); sentence length VERY SHORT to her sister, MEDIUM and polished to a guest; formality LOW then HIGH; vocabulary plain and slightly rough on the surface. Her sister describes her as 「ちょっと不良っぽくて怖い」 and 「嫌いというよりは、苦手」 — EN must let her be unpleasant WITHOUT being a villain.
- The address form 「あんた」 vs 「絵梨奈」 is the project's most-tracked single variable: SEVEN branches, seven different handlings, and each must stay distinct in EN -> Q037, Q291. 0000057B:11:89 is her first on-screen use of the bare given name; 00000EDF:37:161-172 makes it the beat of a 394-cell phone call and her sister answers 「初めてだよ。ずっと『あんた』だけだった」; 00000F0D has it as her plain standard form with the あんた register never appearing; 00000F87:11:240 switches to it on screen mid-confession; 00001755/00001AB5 shows it is the EARLIEST form, from before the roughness started (「よしよし、絵梨奈はいい子だね」); 00002450 has her using it and denying it in the same scene (「覚えてない」).
- LATER (as of 00000E7B): SHE NARRATES A WHOLE 2637-LINE FILE in 私, aged 24, four years after her sister's death, and the あんた register does not appear ONCE — do not import the roughness. Also narrates 0000242E (order 411). The SECOND-PERSON REGISTER at 00000E7B:11:21-155 is unlike anything else in the project: です・ます addressed directly to 「あなた」, the reader, with two numbered questions put to them, essayistic asides on war and economics, and a worked English-language digression (「確か英語では"helpless"という1単語で表される概念です。」 / 「"A little baby is helpless."」) glossed in the next cell. It drops into plain-form 私 narration at about 11:156 with no marker and never returns -> Q230, Q231.
- THE TUMOUR is her only sustained figure and the spine of that narration: 良心 as a 腫瘍 that is 良性 or 悪性, 肥大, fed by 好意 as a 「質の悪い添加物」, 苗床, then 寛解 and 再発 (11:194-206, 28:107-111, 34:45-48, 40:76, 48:292, 82:60-62, 82:170). Keep it MEDICAL in EN and do not vary the vocabulary. Her one other figure, used twice and inverted, is 「青から赤へ、冷から熱へ」 / 「赤から青へ、熱から冷へ」 (0000242E:11:169, 11:177) — both halves must use the same four English words -> Q913.
- Her narration vocabulary is UNEXPECTEDLY EDUCATED (聴覚野, 神経細胞, 情報処理, 寛解, 執念深い, used correctly to build a testable hypothesis; she also has English and glosses a proverb back at her boss). 絵梨奈's narration always treated her as the unacademic sister and the text quietly contradicts it without anyone remarking on it -> Q745.
- THE THING SHE WILL NOT SAY: she reaches for affection and substitutes something else, every time. 「そういうことは、電話では言わない主義」 answers 「お姉ちゃんが好き」, then 「ねえ、絵梨奈」 / 「…………／おやすみ」 (00000EDF:37:357-370); 「だってさ……／あんたのために使えるなら……」 is cut off with 「ああもう！　何でもない！」 (00000F87:11:314); the apology after a failed phone call arrives as a PHOTOGRAPH of a handwritten note, 「役立たずでごめん。元気出して。姉より」, and the ten-year-old reports she was crying (00000EDF:202:148-227). EN must not let the substitution read as coldness -> Q298, Q299.
- She is FUNNY ON PURPOSE in exactly two places and nowhere else: a forty-cell straight-faced routine about her own chest (00000EDF:37:233-286) and the money jokes after the crying at 00000F87:11:297-303 -> Q292.
- Her fullest self-account (00000F87:11:87-215): a boyfriend at 15, dead in an accident inside a month; his high-achieving family stared at her naturally pale hair at the funeral and she could not put her hands together; she stopped seeing the point of being clever and left school; she has worked every day for five years to save enough to leave home. The REAL reason comes only after the false one — 「私、あんたに捨てられるのが怖かった」 and 「絵梨奈にとって、私っていらないんじゃないかって……」. The break itself is shown at 0000205F and the two scenes are never joined -> Q862.
- ALIAS: 藤野桃子 (00000E7B:34:31-32) — she begins 「あ、五島……」 and substitutes it mid-cell, and four characters use the alias for the rest of the file -> Q232. In that file she is named as the 主人公 of the whole thing, in the third person, in the last cell -> Q245.
- Open queries on her: Q037, Q075, Q182, Q230, Q231, Q232, Q245, Q291, Q292, Q298, Q299, Q707, Q743, Q745, Q746, Q767, Q780, Q781, Q862, Q901, Q912, Q913, Q945.

### 伊勢大二郎 (いせ だいじろう, Daijiro Ise) — 警部補 at 元木警察署
- Mentioned from 00000024:51:11 as 伊勢さん; first on screen 000003B4:8:0, first named on screen 000004A7:11:41. The only adult in the story who takes the three girls seriously and the only one who apologises for failing them. As-of: 00002450.
- PRONOUN IS A TELL AND MUST BE TRACKED: 私 is the uniform, 俺 is the man. 私 when announcing himself at a stranger, through a locked door, on the telephone to someone he has not met, and in the briefing register; 俺 with the three girls, with a subordinate, and in all 265 cells of his own narration. From 000004BB onward 俺 is dominant. The switch happens across a single scene boundary at 00001830:8:40-41 (私 for the whole of a call, 俺 in the two cells after she hangs up).
- SIX registers: (1) BRIEFING — flat bureaucratic 丁寧, です・ます, numbered points, statistics, NO contractions in EN; (2) COMMAND — plain-form, barked, 「～だ！」「～してくれ！」「おい！」, purest in an arrest announcement; (3) FLUSTERED — stammering that DOUBLES THE FIRST SYLLABLE of specific words including his own surname and rank (「わわわ私は警察官だ！」, 「わわわわ私は、いいいいせ！」, 「ババババババカを言うな！」 six times in one word at 0000171B:8:39), plus self-justification nobody asked for and blushing. EN must stutter on the SAME words, not generically, and must reproduce a word broken across three cells (「しゅ／しゅ／しゅ／出身は、」) -> Q352; (4) GENTLE — mumbling, hedging, sentences left unfinished, an exit offered three times, described as 聞き取りにくい and ぼそぼそ; he explicitly separates 「警察官として」 from 「俺個人の考え」 before speaking (00000550:11:21-28); (5) WRITTEN — terse plain-form instruction with no softeners and no rank (「30秒でいい。なんとか耐えてくれ。」), structurally the SAME shape as 古郡良治's letter register, and he addresses the girls as 古郡さん／新村さん／五島さん in writing; (6) LATER (as of 00002450:18) the WORKING-THROUGH register — quiet, unhurried, half-joking, with a subordinate, and none of the other five present.
- Particles: ～のだ／～のである in the rant; ～だ！; ～かな softening a question; ～てくれ. Copula だ in dialogue, である in the rant. Tics: 「くそ……」 (his profanity ceiling, the same word and ceiling as 春花's, extended by one notch to 「ちくしょうーー！！」); 「ごほん……」 to recover himself; 「うむむ……」 while thinking; 「いかにも！」; he smokes with the window open when he has finished being a policeman.
- THE 元木美少女同好会 RANT is his fixed comic set piece and it recurs almost verbatim across unrelated branches: 「原理原則」「言語道断」「断じて」, 美少女, 愛でる, 健全な育成, delivered at inappropriate targets, once running ~160 characters in one cell with no break and no closing quotation mark -> Q049. The joke is that a competent, hard-working, well-liked officer has ONE ridiculous and strictly NON-PREDATORY obsession, and the text says so. EN must land him as a comic figure, not a threat -> Q048 — and the comedy must not eat the competence: in 00000EAF the rant is the reason 五島 gets to the final.
- EN correlates: contraction rate LOW in registers 1-2, NONE in the rant, HIGH in the stammering, MEDIUM and broken in the gentle one; profanity ceiling LOW ("damn"); sentence length MEDIUM in the briefing, VERY SHORT when barking, runaway when ranting, fragmentary when gentle; vocabulary SPLIT — real police procedure (再犯率, 示談, 監視カメラ, 巡査部長, 非常線, 緊急逮捕, 死体遺棄, 隠語 ホシ／マルタイ, 居場所, 保護, 尋問, 素行) against comic-absurd hobby vocabulary. Keep the two layers separable.
- LATER (as of 00000F0D:56:0-264): HE NARRATES, 265 cells in 俺, a whole row-block inserted between two blocks of a 15-year-old's first person with NO MARKER at either seam. Translators of that file must not assume the frame is unchanged -> Q369, Q030, Q078. His narration voice: plain past, short declaratives, procedural; he reports what he decided and why in the order he decided it, names his own doubt without dramatising it (「正直、とても悩んでいる」), and asks himself questions he does not answer. NONE of the FLUSTERED register and none of the rant reaches the narration — the comic figure disappears the moment he holds the 俺. The last nine cells drop to one clause each. EN must not make this lyrical and must not make it hard-boiled.
- The GENTLE register at its furthest (00000F0D:20:78-96, 26:139-145): he states his own working rule out loud — the runaways he takes in have all lost a place to belong, and forcing the story out of them fixes nothing — then applies it by refusing to question her, twice, with 「これは取り調べじゃなくて、雑談なんだが……」. THE SAME FILE has him bug the phone he lends her and never mention it. The text does not reconcile the two and EN must not tip either way -> Q350.
- His method, stated in his own words LATER (as of 00002450:18): give the absurd version first and defend it afterwards — 「これは俺の妄想だと思って聞いてくれ」, then the hypothesis, then the evidence. He calls it 妄想 five times while being right, which is the 五島 move in a different mouth. Keep every hedge -> Q943, Q947, Q948, Q949.
- Recurring cross-branch constants with no shared history between them: the FLUSTERED register fires at exactly the same triggers in unrelated branches; 五島 lands on 「おじさん」 for him in three separate branches and he reacts the same way each time; he is the adult still doing the job after the case closes (a press blackout for the three girls, advice to visit a mother before sentencing, a helicopter argued out of a mayor). He also REFUSES TO USE 夏菜's NAME for a whole file, addressing her as 君 even after she objects, shouts it while searching for her, and mouths it silently through a closing train door where it is never heard -> Q354.
- Open queries on him: Q030, Q048, Q049, Q055, Q059, Q078, Q079, Q350, Q352, Q354, Q369, Q643, Q691, Q705, Q711, Q712, Q943, Q947, Q948, Q949.

### navigator (000000F2) — the menu voice; 立木三日 (a 偽名); LATER 新村春花
- The system/menu voice, present from 000000F2 (the earliest file) and speaking to the player and to nobody else. She guides the player through the scenarios in a fixed order, records progress, and explains the scenario board, the リファレンス, the 事典 and the 雑談. Menus, help screens and end-of-route commentary are all her. As-of: 00002330.
- Pronoun: わたくし everywhere. She uses 私 in her self-introduction block and in her cracks. Copula でございます. Speech level: 最上級敬語 (ございます, ～でございます, ～くださいませ) -> Q007.
- EN correlates: contraction rate NONE; profanity ceiling ZERO; sentence length MEDIUM to LONG with heavy nominalisation; formality maximal and unvarying; vocabulary hotel-concierge Japanese applied to mass murder. THE HORROR IS THAT THE REGISTER NEVER MOVES. Keep the English IDENTICAL across every menu file.
- ADDRESS FORMS: the player is always <name>様. The cast are always BARE FULL NAMES with no honorific — 五島絵梨奈, 新村春花, 古郡なつみ, 古郡茜, 新村美冬, 五島桃子, 新村栄一郎, 古郡良治. She is the ONLY voice in the project that uses no honorific for any of them.
- STRUCTURAL VOCABULARY that must be FIXED and reused verbatim: 終焉 for a route ending (counted: 最初の, 2つ目の…), 分岐点 for a branch point, 編 for an arc, 『もしも』の世界 for a what-if, and the handover formula 「～編では知り得なかった、より深き彼らの業をご覧ください」, which is printed identically in three consecutive files (menu text reused, not a replay) -> Q716, Q025.
- THE RULE, laid down at 000015C2:61:16-28 and restated at 0000198D:111:0-5, is the frame of the whole work: the player's one obligation is 「登場人物たちの幸せな結末を見届けること」, and 「あなたが真相を知る必要はなく、真相を知る意思はルールに反します」. THE EN WORDING MUST BE IDENTICAL IN BOTH PLACES -> Q684.
- She REFUSES four times to let the player look at something they have already seen, always flat and always with an apology in it -> Q702, Q703, Q751. She runs EIGHT mutually exclusive player theories about the culprit in one file and THREE mutually exclusive readings of 荒田集落 in another, praising each in turn and committing to none -> Q700.
- HER REGISTER CRACKS, and the cracks are her only characterisation before the reveal: ～ですわ four times; 「あの…………」 and 「やはり、ちょっと緊張しております」 when invited to chat; 「ほほほほほ」 when the player jokes; 「ふふ」 when she cuts herself off -> Q699, Q724. Everything else is identical ございます.
- She WEARS 能面 AND 赤装束 (the 荒田集落 funeral dress, worn by someone outside the story), is accused of being the killer, and denies it flatly, saying there are 「見ていただきたくない身体的特徴」 (000017B6:82:5-8) -> Q702, Q043. She also DENIES the green eye while having one (00001C43:71:0-3), saying eye colour changes with illness or injury.
- She CLAIMS TO HAVE MADE THE WORLD once — 「この世界はわたくしが作った世界、わたくしの案内通りに導かれる世界」 (00001B47:60:2) — and nothing explains it at that point -> Q754.
- LATER (as of 00001CA0:144:0-3): SHE IS 新村春花 AND SHE SAYS SO. 立木三日 is an admitted 偽名; the face she would not show is the blood and the green eye; the world is what her refusal to accept 古郡なつみ's death produced; and the player was invited into it to share a happy ending she could not review alone. The blocks stay separate ONLY so the two registers are kept apart in EN — the identity is ASSERTED, not implied. Cross-reference 新村春花 -> Q763, Q682.
- LATER: she LEAKS the first person three files before the reveal — 「私の母」, 「私が幼少の頃に耽っていた呪い」, 「母」, and a whole cell containing only 「なつみ……」 -> Q820; and says 「わたくしの父、新村栄一郎」 outright at 00002330:77:5 -> Q894.
- LATER: three DIFFERENT KINDS of crack. 00001C8C loses ございます, わたくし and 様 entirely for a whole file (「いいから！　もうそれ以上はやめろ！」, 「黙れ！」); 00001CA0:64:10-11 switches to 私 for two cells at the memory of the killing; 00001EB4:83:2 produces ONE cell of 新村春花's ordinary boyish タメ口 — 「って、バカかよ！　んなわけあるか！」 — apologised for as 「素の話し方」. At her goodbye she names the costume as a costume in her own words: 「最後くらい、ちゃんと私らしく、私の言葉で挨拶したいと思います」, and the next cells are 私 and タメ口 -> Q764, Q765.
- TYPESETTING CHANGES THAT ARE PART OF HER: her lines are printed BARE in every early navigator file and are typeset in 「」 from 00001EA4 onward, with nothing explaining it, coinciding with her having a face -> Q766; one later file prints six cells bare again in one block and returns to 「」 in the next -> Q893; and five of her cells in two files are DUMP MOJIBAKE rather than text -> Q872.
- THE FALSE ENDING (00001C68, 00001C7B): she thanks the player, declares the happy ending reached, dismisses them outright with 「では、さようなら」, and on their return treats them as a new visitor and says 「どうぞ、お引き取り下さい」. Everything after it exists because the player refuses -> Q812.
- Note: her 最上級敬語 is the SAME surface form as 五島桃子's professional register at プリマベラ (000005C1). The two must NOT sound identical in EN -> Q075.

### 新村栄一郎 (にいむら えいいちろう, Eiichiro Niimura) — 春花's adoptive father, dead for the whole story present
- Named from 00000024:47; on screen and speaking first at 000007D3:11:41; alive only in orders 62-66 and dead at 0000082F:11:156, aged about 30. He then appears POSTHUMOUSLY in at least eight different modes, and the gap between the living register and the posthumous ones is the single largest character-handling problem in the project. As-of: 00002473.
- Pronoun: 僕, always, never 俺 — including alone with 古郡良治, the one place his register drops. Stated MORE often than typical and almost always as the subject of a self-criticism. The ONE pronoun switch he has anywhere is under the drug at 000010DD:11:268-269 (「今の僕なら――／俺なら――。」), fourteen cells of 俺, then back to 僕 with no comment -> Q447.
- Speech level: です・ます to 金井美冬 through the courtship and after the marriage; タメ口 to 古郡良治 alone and to 新村サクラ; 丁寧-warm to everyone else. He never uses 呼び捨て on anyone except 良治 and サクラ.
- Particles: ～んです (his commonest ending BY FAR, and always attached to an apology or a self-assessment); ～かな; ～よ gentle to his wife; ～ねー stretched, only to a baby. Copula です.
- THREE separable comic devices in the living register, and they must be kept apart: the STAMMER doubles the FIRST mora and then mangles the vowel (「だだだだいじょうび……！」, 「びょ僕と、家族になってください！」); the PANIC LISP turns consonants into small kana under pressure (「あにょ！」, 「飲めるものはありまひゅか！？」, 「ひゃめて下さい」); the SOB is a groan before it is a sob (「う……／うう……」 then 「ぐす……」 then 「うぐ……ひっく……」), four times in five files. Plus 「気合いだ！　気合い！」 as self-ignition and 「あちちち！」 with hot food.
- EN correlates: contraction rate LOW (he is formal even when broken); profanity ceiling ZERO — his strongest word is 「くそう」 once, about his own face; sentence length SHORT, and almost every sentence is an apology, a self-deprecation or a question about someone else's comfort; formality HIGH and never relaxing; vocabulary plain and unliterary with ONE repeated abstract phrase, 「家族になる」, which must be the SAME EN words every time and must NEVER become "get married".
- Physical facts the EN must not smooth: short and heavily overweight, said repeatedly in his own voice and others'; he drags one leg and explains it once as a childhood injury, which 古郡良治 privately doubts; and he climbs a large tree with no difficulty. LATER (as of 000010DD) the leg is explained — he made ドローガ from the family books to protect 新村サクラ, tested it on himself, and broke his own leg in a compound fracture -> Q452, Q460.
- STRUCTURAL JOB: he is the only adult in the story who is kind without an agenda, and the text kills him in one cell. The comedy around his body is his own and everyone else's affection; it must NEVER read as the narration mocking him.
- Narration: he narrates 000010DD whole (~370 cells, 僕, his first narration anywhere in 154 files; nothing names him until 11:45) and an unframed inset at 00000F24:187:11-37 -> Q448, Q400. His NARRATION VOICE is short plain-past sentences, heavy ellipsis, and a question asked of himself roughly every fifth cell — the ～んです self-criticism turned inward, relentless, auditing his own motive on screen and reaching 「僕は、最低な奴だ……」. IMPORTANT: at fourteen and fifteen the STAMMER, the PANIC LISP and the ～んです are ALL ABSENT in dialogue as well as narration, so the comic surface of 000007D3 is something he ACQUIRED later, not something he lost.
- POSTHUMOUS MODE 1, the GENTLE one (00000E31, a whole file; 0000151C:8:342-378; 00001C08; 0000136D-000013CD in part; 00001D6F:8:111-139): 僕, 「はは」, the gentleness and the 「家族」 thinking survive; the STAMMER, the PANIC LISP, the ～んです self-criticism and the apologies are GONE. He is calm, brief and in charge of the conversation. EN must not reach for his comic register here and must not make him solemn either.
- POSTHUMOUS MODE 2, the ANTAGONIST (00000F24:142:188-187:78; 000014D7; 00002340; 00002444:32; 00002473:11:220-315): 僕, 「はは」/「はっはっは！」, 美冬ちゃん／茜ちゃん／なつみちゃん／五島ちゃん／茅萱ちゃん and bare 春花 survive; NOTHING ELSE DOES. What replaces them is a level, unhurried, faintly amused plain form with mock-fatherly correction in it, and the narration names the voice before it names him: 「聞き覚えがあるはずなのに、親しみを感じない邪な声」. THE CRUELTY IS ALL UNDERSTATEMENT — 「あーあ……やっぱり出来損ないだったか」 of his wife's corpse, 「ははは、哀れだよねえ」 of a death, 「まあいいや。とりあえず行こう」. He NEVER raises his voice and NEVER swears. The ONE thing he apologises for is a scheduling error in a murder, and the ～んです shape of his old self-criticism is visible underneath it with the object changed.
- THE CONSTRAINT, and it is hard: the identity is ASSERTED, not implied — his wife says 「栄一郎さん」, he says 「父親」, a character misidentifies him as his twin and is corrected. This is the same character, not a double. EN must NOT hedge it and must NOT reach for his comic register. A translator meeting him in one of those files alone will have no idea what has been lost -> Q390. He himself REFUSES to settle it once: 「僕が栄一郎だろうが兄貴だろうが、君には一緒に来てもらわないといけない」 -> Q570.
- Address forms unchanged across twelve years and one death: なつみちゃん, 美冬ちゃん, 良治 bare, サクラ bare, 母さん, 兄貴 for 幸太郎 (NEVER 幸太郎), and his own daughter is 春花 bare, never 春花ちゃん — which is how she identifies him at 00002444:32:27-29.
- He is the only character who states outright that the story's central question must not be answered (00000E31), and he extracts a promise on it -> Q219. He also ABSOLVES 古郡なつみ of the twelve years of guilt this project is built on, in two broken cells at 0000151C:8:363-364, and the narration does not comment.
- Untagged/unframed passages he is involved in: 00000FCF:47:1-2 (two closing cells, unattributed, 僕 the only identification, 母さん his only address form, nothing frightening in it) -> Q429, Q047; 000014D7, where he is HALF the chunk's exposition in untagged 「」 cells alternating with 新村サクラ for about sixty cells, attributable only by 僕 and plain form against her feminine endings -> Q592; 00002444:32:245-259, terms given in alternating untagged cells with her.
- Open queries on him: Q047, Q117, Q219, Q386, Q390, Q400, Q429, Q447, Q448, Q452, Q454, Q460, Q496, Q570, Q592, Q624, Q802, Q895, Q977, Q978, Q980, Q981, Q982.

### 新村茅萱 (にいむら ちがや, Chigaya Niimura) — 春花's cousin, 幸太郎's elder daughter, ~25, a university student in Tokyo
- First appears 00000944:8:16, silent on a train; first line 8:23; named at 8:41. One of the project's biggest narrators (an eleven-file continuous run, plus several more). As-of: 00002478.
- Pronoun: 私, typical frequency. Copula よ／わ, feminine. Dialect: none.
- Speech level: SOFT FEMININE タメ口 to everyone including adults; she refuses both to be given and to give 敬語 (「私たち、先輩でも後輩でもないでしょ？」). Particles ～のよ／～の (constant), ～わよ, ～ね closing almost every line, ～かしら. The ONE 敬語 she uses in the whole project is the written MEMO register at 0000136D (~25 cells of です・ます addressed to an unknown reader, calm, apologising for the handwriting).
- 「ん……」 OPENS NEARLY EVERY LINE SHE SPEAKS, in every register, and it is the single most load-bearing tic in the project. It is attested from about age five to twenty-five, across ELEVEN branches with no shared history, through a fourth-generation conversion and back out of it again. It is ABSENT from her narration and PRESENT in every spoken line, including shouted ones, ceremonial ones, orders, threats and lines spoken into the floor. It is frequently the ONLY proof of identity the text gives a reader. FIX ONE EN RENDERING AND USE IT EVERYWHERE -> Q138, Q091. It is also the exact tic of the unnamed caller at 00000697:8:61-87, and the text never confirms they are the same person -> Q091.
- ITS ABSENCE IS ALWAYS AN EVENT and there are only four recorded: 「お父さん！　もうやめて！／私は……／お父さんの操り人形じゃない！」 (00000B9F:11:108-110); 「は？」 (00001325:8:151), one syllable with no softness, at being told her own name is hard to read -> Q530; the whole of her collapse at 00001C5D:11:150-169 -> Q806; and the INTERIOR SCREAM, three bare cells of 「キエエエ……！！」, the first ~80 characters long, which has no register at all -> Q527. EN must find a way to show the absence.
- Other tics: 「うふふ」; 「ダッセエ」 split across three cells; a wail 「ううう……！」 before she cries.
- EN correlates: contraction rate LOW; profanity ceiling ZERO IN WORDS and MEDIUM IN CONTENT — this is the whole EN problem with her, see the INSTRUCTOR register below; sentence length SHORT and unhurried; formality LOW but never rude; vocabulary plain, with drinking and socialising nouns (コンパ, 合コン, 連絡先, バイト).
- THE GAP BETWEEN HOW SHE LOOKS AND WHAT SHE DOES IS THE CHARACTER: the narration spends six cells on her beauty (pale skin, long straight hair, blue-and-green ODD EYES, "beauty made into a shape") and then she flags down a train with her thumb, picks 山菜 off the track side, weeps about men who stop answering her, and shows photographs of herself force-feeding a man beer. なつみ lands on 「残念な美人」. EN must let the first paragraph be sincere so the second can land.
- Registers: (1) the sober soft one; (2) DRUNK — every vowel stretched, volume up, 「あはははははは！」, and a witness says her eyes are those of a different person; (3) the 魔女 register — in 能面 and 赤装束 with a 鉈 and a メイス she says NOTHING AT ALL, bows, and leaves without looking at anyone. Her silence in costume is total, like the 死神's — do not give her a sound; (4) the LEADER register — the tic survives and the softness does not: flat orders, role assignment before anyone asks; (5) the STORYTELLER register (00000A18:11:6-306, ~295 cells of first-person 丁寧 with no 「」) — the tic DISAPPEARS inside the story and survives only where she quotes her own speech; level, unhurried, one fact per line, no jokes; (6) the INSTRUCTOR register, the coldest she is anywhere — she wins fights and annotates them while winning, twice stating 「私、暴力が嫌いなの」 while pinning a relative to the floor, and she MEANS IT. EN must keep the softness and the tic and let the content do the damage; (7) the MOTHER-SUBSTITUTE register, comic, ～なさい imperatives and the tic on every line, all of it play; (8) the RESEARCH register — two dictionaries, photographs of what she cannot read, a work split set out in advance. Competence, not genius, and the file says so.
- HER PHYSICAL HABIT WITH 五島絵梨奈 is constant, one-sided and in every branch: she puts her face against a sleeping girl's, strokes her chin, holds her hand, shares a seatbelt, carries her off, and says 「今の私の生きがいは五島ちゃんだけ！」. It is played as comedy and 五島 is genuinely distressed. Do NOT make it menacing and do NOT make it cute. Her address form moves 五島さん → 五島ちゃん inside single files.
- Narration: she narrates an eleven-file continuous run (000012F5, 0000130D, then orders 178-185, 00001325 through 000013CD) — her longest and the second longest by anyone — plus orders 156-163 (0000110D through 000011B4), 0000154E, 00001EFF, 00002433, 00002439, and 00002478, THE LAST FILE OF THE PROJECT -> Q030, Q078, Q461, Q616, Q983. Narration voice: soft, plain past, one thought per cell, with the same ～のよ／～かしら shape her speech has, self-aware and dry about her own failures, and COMPLETELY FLAT for the murders — she narrates 「私が殺したのは、全部で5人――」 in the same register as a hangover.
- 000012F5 has NO DIALOGUE AT ALL — 186 lines of one woman reading in a storehouse — and it is the only such file anywhere.
- THE CONFESSION SHE MAKES IN SEVERAL BRANCHES: at eleven she overheard a woman planning to take her father to the police and put a knife through her throat that night, then killed one person each April for five years. Her motive is entirely protective. She is then TOLD SHE IS WRONG — the 呪殺 corpses were all beaten to death with a blunt instrument, not stabbed — and nobody has an explanation -> Q461, Q462, Q477. LATER (as of 00001EFF:8:21-68) the answer arrives and it undoes nine years: the condition was set IN A DREAM, she killed IN THE DREAM, she did not know it was a dream, and 「実際に、私が誰かを殺すことはなかった」. She indicts herself anyway, for having been willing. LATER (as of 00002439:11:166-168) she makes the SAME confession as a deliberate LIE to stop her sister and is slapped for it -> Q927.
- Her ABILITY is the same seasonal cherry precognition as 古郡なつみ's, present since she was ten, told to nobody, because at 荒田 the gift is called 『魔女の力』 and blamed on her mother -> Q478. Her ODD EYES are one blue and one green, the blue from her mother and the green from nowhere; her sister's are the same pair. THE 天秤: she says she has carried an unfairly weighted set of scales for nine years, and at 00001184:24:2-22 the scales TALK to her in 僕 and 君 and mock her out of her own confession, verdict 「空。」 (own CAST block) -> Q463.
- Her best piece of thinking, WRITING BLIND (0000136D:8:15-61): she reasons that the thing has her senses but not her thoughts, starts a voice recording, and writes a memo with her eyes deliberately turned away so what she writes cannot be read through her — then finds one sentence wrong and cannot correct it, because correcting it means looking -> Q542, Q543. Her named method is 「歴史は繰り返す、2度あることは3度ある」 / 「私の温故知新」, and it is a DIFFERENT instrument from 五島's.
- The DECEPTION register (0000139D through 000014D7, seven files): she announces the performance in narration before she gives it, escalates on a stated schedule, and notes that her vocabulary being poor made it more convincing. She twice says the muttering began to become a habit -> Q549. LATER: the conversion at 00001414-0000142B is part of the same performance, on written instruction, and THE REGISTER DOES NOT CHANGE — the tic, the soft ～のよ／～わ and 五島ちゃん are all intact in the threatening cells and only the content moves. EN must not cool the voice -> Q569.
- Open queries on her: Q030, Q078, Q091, Q137, Q138, Q262, Q461-Q464, Q466, Q477, Q478, Q527, Q530, Q541-Q543, Q549, Q551, Q555, Q569, Q616, Q806, Q817, Q837, Q917, Q924, Q927, Q983, Q986, Q988.

### 新村夏菜 (にいむら かな, Kana Niimura) — 春花's cousin, 茅萱's younger sister, 幸太郎's second daughter, 10-12 (14-16 in two later branches)
- First appears 0000095B:35:36. Age varies by branch: 10 in most, 11 in some, 12 in one, and 14-16 in 00000E7B and 00000F0D, which are set four years later. As-of: 00002478.
- Pronoun: 私, typical frequency. Copula だ. Dialect: none.
- Speech level baseline: OVER-FAMILIAR タメ口 with no distance at all, to adults and strangers alike, no 敬語 anywhere — AND THIS BASELINE IS BROKEN IN TWO WHOLE FILES, see below. Particles: ～じゃん; ～し; ～の！？ stacked (her question-storm form); ～からね！; ～よねー.
- Tics: she introduces herself by full name five times in five cells, each with a different relationship attached; she fires fifteen personal questions in fifteen cells with no pause; she ECHOES the last words of other people's lines, one echo per cell -> Q142; 「ほーかほーか！」; 「おもらししてない？」; a STAMMER in which the whole mora repeats and multiplies (「ままままま待ってよ！」, 「だだだだだだから読まないでって言ったの！») — unlike 新村栄一郎's it does NOT mangle the vowel, and it is pure embarrassment, never panic.
- EN correlates: contraction rate HIGH; profanity ceiling LOW-MEDIUM in a very specific way — she is ten and says 「ロリ巨乳」, 「まな板」 and worse, and THE SHOCK IS THAT A CHILD SAYS THEM; sentence length VERY SHORT; formality ZERO; vocabulary a ten-year-old's plus internet and adult words she has clearly picked up rather than understood. She uses ちゃん on everyone she attacks — 春花ちゃん, 五島ちゃん, なつみちゃん, and on the antagonist she is helping to beat. KEEP THE MISMATCH between the suffix and the content; it never stops contradicting itself.
- THE QUIET REGISTER is her second voice and it is the hinge of every file she is in: she drops every ～じゃん, every stacked question and every adult word, and lists things in a cheerful surface while the sadness sits in what she lists (00000AF7:11:85-127; 00000E7B:40:79-91; 00000EDF:186:76-89; 00000F24:88:154-262; 0000148E:8:189). EN MUST NOT MAKE HER PITIABLE — the lines are cheerful on the surface and nobody in the scene names the loneliness but the narrator.
