- Two branches break the no-敬語 rule completely and BOTH are explained in-text: in 00000E7B she speaks です・ます to one addressee across 2637 lines without one lapse, and the politeness is what carries the horror (「桃子さん、私、もうすぐママを殺すんです」, 「だったら、今すぐ私をここで撃ち殺してください」) — do not let EN politeness soften any of them. In 00000F0D です・ます is her baseline to EVERY adult, and she explains it at 20:103: her mother told her at the start of middle school to use 敬語 to adults she does not know AND to stop the crude talk. The two rules are stated together, which makes the politeness and the missing sexual-joke register ONE change rather than two. Her タメ口 returns at [68] for one addressee only, and the drop is the relationship changing, not the character loosening.
- She is PHYSICALLY EXTRAORDINARY and the text mostly does not remark on it: she drops out of the top of a several-hundred-year-old cherry and out of a ceiling void; she goes through the trees 「まるでムササビのように」 with her eyes shut; she hunts and kills with a nata without a word; she smells gunpowder across a clearing and a storehouse on her sister's clothes from across a room; she reads a stranger's骨格 for fighting strength in one cell. Explanations offered are 「あいつの運動神経おかしいからな」 and 五島 calling it trainable parkour.
- HER REASONING IS IN A CHILD'S GRAMMAR AND IT IS NOT A CHILD'S REASONING, and this is the hardest thing to get right in EN. Examples: 「なつみちゃん、10年前に女ケ沢で何したの？　ママを殺そうとしたの？」 / 「だったらなつみちゃんは悪くないじゃない」; the 死生観 argued out at 00001B70:11:130-206 (killing is wrong only because of the bereaved, therefore killing a whole family is fine, therefore 「だから死刑があるんじゃないの？」); the MARRIAGE-ECONOMICS lecture at 000012C5:8:142-174, sourced from web novels read with her grandmother, with not one childish marker in it and 五島 losing — EN must NOT make this precocious-cute -> Q500; and 「死ぬのが怖くないのと、死んでもいいのは違うでしょ？」, the cleanest reasoning anyone does in its file.
- She COINS 「エリ姉」 on the spot for 五島絵梨奈 — independently, in at least three separate branches with no shared history, in one cell each, the same kin-suffix shape as チガ姉 — and in two other branches she never coins it at all, and in one she ASKS for permission instead -> Q171, Q498.
- Narration: she narrates ~2,300 of the 2,717 cells of 00000F0D (order 138) in 私 -> Q030, Q078, and a whole file as a PRE-VERBAL INFANT (00001C34, 私, present tense, no words, identifying her sister by feel and placing herself by sound, closing 「やがて私は、泣き疲れ、眠り、すべてを忘れ――／ハイハイで探し続けるのだろう。／永遠に」). It is the project's only pre-verbal narrator and it is NOT framed as recollection -> Q768. Her narration voice is NOT the ～じゃん question-storm: plain past, one thought per cell, heavy on self-interrogation and COUNTING (money, times, kilometres, minutes), reasoning in numbered options. Her ordinary spoken register survives inside it unchanged — EN must let the narration be level and the dialogue over-familiar.
- Special narration modes in 00000F0D: the JOKE-TELLER register, where she plays a naive runaway on purpose and NARRATES the performance as she gives it and rates her own acting — two levels in one paragraph; the HUNTER register, flat and unhedged, competence stated without boasting; the DELIBERATION register (50:29-70, 179:84-93), the coldest thing in the file, reasoning toward killing a named person with the cells ALTERNATING BETWEEN TWO VOICES ARGUING, both hers, with no tags and no quotation marks — EN must not resolve which voice is which; and the COLLAPSE register in [62], where the narration goes SECOND PERSON to people who are not there, lists six abstract nouns one per cell, and uses ～かしら twice, which appears nowhere else in her speech -> Q374.
- Her ABILITY is the same 直感 as 古郡なつみ's, waking for the first time in 00000F0D. Her father states the trigger as 桜 ALONE, where なつみ's needs 桜 and 風. She reads the ABSENCE of a warning as permission and says so.
- Her ODD EYES are one blue and one green, the same pair as her sister's; LATER (as of 0000151C:8:307) both are blue, the green having been the thing all along, and her whole reaction is 「あは！　じゃあもう大丈夫だね！」.
- THE ADDRESS-FORM CAMPAIGN of 00000F0D is that file's spine: she objects to being called 「君」, is refused, raises it twice more, and the file's last beat is her reading her own name off a man's mouth through a closing train door. EVERY 「君」 in the file is load-bearing and none may be translated as her name -> Q354.
- CONTENT NOTE, a handling decision and not a style one: across 00000F0D [26], [68] and [74] she makes repeated romantic advances to an officer roughly twice her age, at 14, 15 and 16, and he refuses every one and states the rule he is keeping. The JP is explicit about both the ages and the refusals. Translate plainly, add nothing, remove nothing, do not warm the wording -> Q368 (owner).
- Open queries on her: Q030, Q078, Q114, Q142, Q171, Q182, Q224, Q351, Q354, Q368, Q374, Q475, Q498, Q500, Q525, Q526, Q768, Q776, Q857, Q860, Q925, Q926.

### 新村幸太郎 (にいむら こうたろう, Kotaro Niimura) — 栄一郎's twin brother, 春花's uncle, 茅萱 and 夏菜's father
- First appears 0000095B:27:7, seen and mistaken for his dead twin's ghost; first line 35:15. Heavily overweight, like his brother, and the settlement's most trusted man and its 長's deputy. As-of: 00002478.
- Pronoun: 俺, LESS often than typical — and ONE unexplained 僕 in the cell immediately after he introduces himself (0000095B:35:22), which was his twin's only pronoun and is never remarked on. LATER (as of 0000246E) he slips into 僕 twice in one interrogation scene.
- Speech level: relaxed タメ口 to everyone including his mother-in-law's guests; 丁寧-ish only for a toast; です・ます under interrogation LATER. Particles ～じゃないか, ～だろ, ～よ (soft). Copula だ.
- HIS LAUGH IS 「はっはっは！」 and it is a structural fact: he laughs INSTEAD of intervening, every single time. It must differ in EN from 「ははは」 (which he also has, smaller) and from 「ほほほ」 (his mother's). Its ABSENCE marks every serious scene he is in — it does not appear once in the TEACHING register, the ANTAGONIST register, the confession files, or any of his narration. He also announces a departure rather than making one (「さて、俺は部屋で寝るよ」).
- EN correlates: contraction rate MEDIUM; profanity ceiling ZERO-PLUS-ONE (くそ and 「バカ野郎……」 once each, and interior 「人間様をなめるなキノコめ！」 as the single strongest thing he says); sentence length SHORT; formality LOW-warm; vocabulary plain. The EN must NOT let him inherit 栄一郎's stammer, his 僕 or his apologies — except for the single 僕 above and the DOMESTIC register below.
- HE HAS SIX REGISTERS and a translator meeting him in one file will meet only one of them:
  - the TEACHING register (00000AC4:11:15-67, his largest early scene): one question per turn, a pause, then the answer named out loud — 起承転結, which part matters, what happens if 起 is wrong. Then he invites them to treat him as the murderer, says he will not interrupt, and keeps to it for thirty cells. This is the 「荒田きっての切れ者」 shown rather than claimed -> Q166.
  - the DRUNK HOST register: relaxed, flushed, no 「はっはっは！」, and he volunteers the most damaging personal fact about himself unasked (that his wife preferred his twin). Embarrassed, not evasive. Do NOT make him sinister here.
  - the DOMESTIC register (00000EDF:47:28-78), his warmest anywhere: whining while being patched up — 「いででで！」, 「うう……やっぱり俺、五島ちゃんに嫌われたよな……。／俺なんて……」 — and his wife's verdict is 「へこみ方は栄一郎そっくりなのね」. The twin's self-abasement surfacing in him -> Q117.
  - the ANTAGONIST register (00000B9F:11:101-334, 00000E19:11:77-187, 00002057, 000020D8, and staged at 00002473): it is the TEACHING register with the warmth taken out, and the narration calls it 「抑揚のない声で淡々と」 and 「優しく諭すように穏やか」 IN THE SAME SCENE. HE NEVER SAYS 殺す OR 死ね, NEVER SHOUTS, NEVER THREATENS, and states demands as offers or as statements of need — 「必要なんだよ、なつみちゃんがね」, 「君がこちらへ来てくれたら、2人はこのままそっちに返すよ」, 「もしここで俺たちに加わらなかったら……／どうなるか分かりますよね？」. Every diminutive stays intact — 五島ちゃん, 春花ちゃん, なつみちゃん, 夏菜 — through two nata swings. 五島 says the absence of those two words is the thing she cannot place. Newer marker: 「くっふふふ……」 and the narration naming the drop in pitch. EN MUST KEEP HIM MILD.
  - the NEGOTIATOR register (00000F0D, telephone only): 「お前がそれを知る必要はない」, getting ahead of a threat before it is made, and trading — 「それはお前が集落に帰ってきてから教える」. His daughter's narration names the technique twice. Four cells later the same call is ordinary and warm, and NEITHER IS A MASK.
  - the WRITTEN register, once (000014D7:8:353-357, five bare cells with the signature alone on the last): plain タメ口, 俺, お前, four short imperatives and 「頼むぞ。」 — structurally the same shape as 古郡良治's and 伊勢's letter registers -> Q593.
- Narration: he narrates a 20-cell inset in someone else's file (00000AC4:19:24-43), the ~90-cell confession that ENDS a chunk (0000154E:8:139-228) -> Q617, a 45-line standalone (00002457) -> Q952, and FOUR CONSECUTIVE FILES, orders 418-421, about 1,040 cells with no marker at any file boundary — his longest by a factor of ten -> Q964. NARRATION VOICE, identical in all of them: plain sequential past, short declaratives, one thought per cell, heavy ellipsis, NO adjectives, NO self-defence and NO self-pity, and a question asked of himself every few cells (「俺は何をしているのだろうか」). He never says what he felt. Figurative language is rationed to roughly one phrase per file and the notes list them individually.
- HIS MOTIVE, stated plainly and repeatedly once he narrates, and every consequence in the project runs out of it: he protects the thing because stopping means never seeing his dead wife again. 「それに俺までここを離れてしまったら、サクラに会えなくなるじゃないか！」, 「でないと、サクラとは2度と……」, and the unfinished last cell 「これで、サクラとは永遠に……」. He names the cost to others and does not claim it excuses anything: 「これまで犠牲になった人たちには申し訳ないと思っている」.
- HIS OWN VERDICTS ON HIMSELF are three short sentences, all in narration: 「俺は弱かった」, 「俺は、ずっと死月妖花に騙され続けていた」, and (once, self-exculpating, and he is struck for it) 「俺はサクラに……あいつらにだまされていたんだ……」.
- THE 沈丁花 are his signature object and they are read two different ways by the text: 000014A5:8:24-28 reads the weekly flowers at his wife's river bank as grief and his younger daughter argues him innocent from them; 0000245F:11:105-106 has him state flatly that they are CAMOUFLAGE. The origin scene (0000245F:11:62-99) is his only tender flashback: she tells him the flower is the opposite of the cherry that carries her name, gives the 花言葉 as 不死、不滅、永遠, and asks him to offer it if she dies -> Q966, Q192.
- HE BREAKS twice and it is the SAME break, printed once from outside and once from inside: pinned under his brother's tree, fed his own happiest memories, he shouts at his dead wife — 「サクラ！　裏切ったのか！／俺はお前のために、ずっとずっとお前のために……！」 — and the narration says his mind is no longer sound. His last named referents in every branch are his brother and his wife.
- THE DOUBLE GAME (00002473) is the answer to a question his block has carried since 00000E19: the villain performance is DELIBERATE and he narrates the staging of it, announcing it before he does it, knocking his own daughter out with the deer-stunning method so as not to injure her, pulling the nata into the ground, and reading 五島 swap a key and giving her the opening. The performance REUSES THE 00000E19 LINES WORD FOR WORD and the EN of both printings must be IDENTICAL -> Q973. Under it his narration says 「『すべては死月妖花の仕業だ』／なんてここで言えたらどれほど楽か」.
- LATER, in a visiting room (00002478), he has NO register left at all: head down, eyes unfocused, swaying, muttering, four coherent cells, and shouted orders that contradict each other in one breath — 「女神を殺せ！」 and 「称えろ！」 — with a stammer on one mora -> Q984, Q985.
- Open queries on him: Q001, Q117, Q166, Q168, Q180, Q192, Q196, Q237, Q454, Q455, Q458, Q593, Q617, Q619, Q631, Q788, Q799, Q800, Q801, Q937, Q950, Q951, Q952, Q964, Q966, Q968, Q973, Q974, Q984, Q985, Q995.

### 新村エリカ (にいむら エリカ, Erika Niimura) — 春花's grandmother, ~70-71, 荒田集落's 長 and its 大魔女
- First appears 0000095B:11:70; first line 11:71. She married into 荒田 in 祀耀750 and is the settlement's oldest resident. As-of: 00002478.
- Pronoun: 私 in her own voice; in an impression she uses the pronoun of whoever she is doing. Copula だ. Dialect: none.
- FOUR THINGS THAT MUST STAY APART IN EN:
  1. THE IMPRESSIONS. She greets visitors dressed and speaking as somebody else in the room — as 春花 (with ～な／～ぜ and 「クソして待ってな」, which the real 春花 never says), as 茅萱 (wig and coloured contacts), and as 五島 (school uniform and twintails, learned from a video, announcing 「自他ともに認める天才少女五島エリカ参上！」). EACH IS ONE NOTCH BROADER THAN THE ORIGINAL, and that is the joke. She is an anime fan with a wardrobe of costumes, and she made 春花 practise an impression of HER as a child, which is where the 00000779 「大魔女 voice」 block comes from -> Q139, Q140, Q109.
  2. HER OWN VOICE: unhurried plain タメ口/丁寧-casual, ～ね／～だね／～かね, one fact per sentence, NO JOKES, NO TICS AT ALL — and that absence is the point. It is the voice the project is mostly read in.
  3. the HOST voice: short, generous, entirely about the guests.
  4. the 大魔女 register (heard for real at 00000A94:11:129-130 and retold at 00001459:8:199-237): 丁寧 carrying the law — 「美冬さん……自分がしたこと、分かりますよね？／掟を破ることは、処罰の対象です」 — DROPPED inside two cells for 「まあ……／いいんじゃないかね！」. THE DROP IS LOAD-BEARING: a woman overheard the first half and not the second and it killed her. The EN of the two halves must be AS FAR APART AS THE JP -> Q575, Q576.
- Tics: 「はっはっは！」 (shared with her son-in-law 幸太郎 — the two must be distinguishable in EN); 「よっしゃ！」; 「おうおう！」; 「むふ」 when a prank has landed. ALL of them are showman markers and ALL of them are absent from her serious files.
- EN correlates: contraction rate MEDIUM in her own voice and WHATEVER THE TARGET USES in an impression; profanity ceiling MEDIUM BUT ONLY INSIDE AN IMPRESSION (zero otherwise); sentence length SHORT; formality LOW to guests and quietly absolute to her own household — she gives orders and gets 「は……／はい……」. The ONE person she gives 敬語 to anywhere is her own mother-in-law (「心得ます」, 「失礼致しました」), which is the same shape 新村美冬 gives her.
- HER DEFINING MOVE: she says the worst thing about herself, flatly, with no defence and no grief word, immediately on being asked, and then stops. Instances: 「50年前、本当にあったことだよ」 recanting her own ghost-story frame; admitting she personally ordered for fifty years that nobody in 荒田 was ever to be stopped from taking their own life, and that she avoided thinking about the reason because thinking was itself a breach; 「私が殺したも同然だね……」 about a death she did not cause; 「いや、結局私は助けられなかった。／私が殺したと言っても――」; 「やっぱり、10年前のことは間違いだったんだよ」 said standing over her own son with his arm off. EN MUST NOT RAISE THE VOICE ANYWHERE IN THESE.
- SHE IS THE MOST PERCEPTIVE PERSON IN THE ROOM and two separate characters name it as a skill: she reads 五島's 呼び捨て complex off two lines of small talk, and 五島 describes the method — 「私のように論理的に考えるのではなく、相手の表情や間を見て、そう察している」. The WITHHOLDING THAT IS NOT A LIE is her best trick and she uses it on a detective and on her own son: she looks down, tilts her head, says she has never heard of the thing, and then offers the same proposition back as a hypothetical. The narration flags that she never said she had forgotten -> Q969.
- She GRANTS HER OWN GIVEN NAME and BANS 敬語 in branch after branch, and the handling differs every time: as a paired order in one scene (「この際だから敬語もなしだ！　私たち、これで友達だ！」); again in a branch with no shared history, with the same corrected-mid-cell compliance; once when the 敬語 has already fallen off by itself so there is nothing to ban; and once phrased through a third party and REFUSED, without her pressing -> Q499, Q561.
- The COMMANDER register, at about seventy: she takes a nata (「仕方ないね……久しぶりに運動するとしようか」), holds a settlement entrance, orders her son below by bare given name while insulting his stomach, puts ten people down WITHOUT hitting a vital point on purpose and explains the restraint flatly, and goes alone through a forest of walking corpses for a child with 「これでも、そこら辺の男には負けやしないさ！」.
- The CONSOLING register, new and her warmest (00001295:8:85-194): she comes in behind a crying stranger, strokes her back, and says nothing until spoken to. It works by NAMING THE OTHER PERSON'S POSITION rather than by reassurance — 「怖くて当たり前だよ。怖いって気持ちがないと、無謀なことをして失敗するだけ」 — and it lands on 「ちゃんと、無事に家に帰してあげるからね」.
- SHE NAMES HERSELF AN INCOMER twice and it is the hinge of her relationship with outsiders: the short form 「私はもともと外の人間でね」 and the long one 「私はね、旦那と結婚する時に決めたんだよ。何があってもこの地を守り抜くって。／それは、この地に住む人はもちろん、ここを訪れたお客人も同じこと」.
- Her one long set-piece narration is the 130-cell first-person 怪談 at 000009A0:11:101-238 (no quotation marks, the only place she is serious in that chunk). She never says who killed the man, ends on 「脅かす者は排除するという、恐怖心と闘争心がね」, and then denies it was true -> Q146.
- SHE IS A REFORMER, not a traditionalist, and this is easy to get backwards: 「呪いなんてもの、ありゃしない。／あるとすれば、それは誰かが裏で手を引いている」; 「ただ伝統を守ることが善じゃない」; she takes the settlement's 掟 apart on screen; she sets one aside to offer refuge; she once intended to leave the settlement rather than have her family refused; and she names her own death under the 75歳自殺 rule as a four-year deadline. It runs ALONGSIDE the fifty-year suicide policy she enforced and NEITHER FILE ACKNOWLEDGES THE OTHER — cite both -> Q914, Q916, Q802.
- SHE IS ALSO A SUBORDINATE, once (00002026): her mother-in-law names her 大魔女 and gives her three transmissions with the rule that none may be written down, and REFUSES HER THE REASON TWICE — 「エリカさんはそこまで踏み込まなくていい」, 「知らなくていい」 — and she apologises. This is the EXACT shape of her own later refusals to others, decades on -> Q777, Q778. The same scene reveals the cosplay apparatus was her mother-in-law's hobby before it was hers -> Q779.
- HER TRANSMISSIONS are quoted material and must be rendered as fixed text: a two-line classical instruction written by 新村桔梗 two 大魔女 ago; a second half released a file later (「ただ翠眼のみが打ち勝つ。／翠眼となりし人々、妖花を枯らし死者を黄泉に返す。／子孫は必ず面と血色の装束をまとい抗うべし」); and an ORAL-ONLY one, in no book, drilled into every 大魔女 「掛け算九九のように」, naming 死神, four 神使, アバドン and a sulphur volcano. Her gate on releasing any of them is purely practical -> Q558, Q567.
- SHE DIES BETWEEN FILES in at least two branches, off screen, of a 心筋梗塞 in May with no 事件性, reported in two cells. LATER (as of 00000FCF) her LAST REGISTER ARRIVES ON THE WIND: four indented one-to-five-cell blocks with no speaker tag, no 「」 and no narration inside them, in her own voice with two archaic imperatives on it, releasing her granddaughter from 荒田 — 「外の世界からでも、荒田を守ることはできる」. Her granddaughter's answer is to accuse the voice of being one of her impressions, which does not settle what it was -> Q428, Q429.
- Open queries on her: Q109, Q139, Q140, Q146, Q174, Q428, Q429, Q430, Q458, Q459, Q499, Q558, Q561, Q567, Q575, Q576, Q750, Q777, Q778, Q779, Q802, Q810, Q811, Q840, Q858, Q914, Q915, Q916, Q968, Q969, Q972.

### 新村サクラ (にいむら サクラ, Sakura Niimura) — 幸太郎's wife, 茅萱 and 夏菜's mother, dead ten years
- Named at 00000A62:19:70; on screen and speaking at 00000A7C:11:4. She is dead before the story present in almost every branch, and most of her material is flashback, recording, or her walking corpse. As-of: 00002473.
- Pronoun: 私, LESS often than typical — she drops subjects and speaks about the other person instead. Copula よ／わ, feminine. Dialect: none.
- Speech level: soft feminine タメ口 to EVERYONE, including a seven-year-old and her mother-in-law's guests. です・ます upward to her mother-in-law and, in one branch, to a guest for a whole file. Particles: ～ね closing most lines, ～の／～のよ, ～かしら, ～よ？ for gentle correction.
- HER OPENING LINE IS ALWAYS A QUESTION ABOUT THE LISTENER. This is her single most reliable identifier, it survives her death, and the notes flag every place it is absent (only where a line has no room for it).
- TICS: NONE, and that is the characterisation — no laugh, no filler, no catchphrase. DO NOT GIVE HER A VERBAL HABIT IN EN. Under strain the only thing she produces is 「ごめんなさい……」 twice in two cells and then a wordless cry.
- EN correlates: contraction rate LOW; profanity ceiling ZERO; sentence length SHORT; formality warm-informal; vocabulary plain and domestic, with the settlement's words (掟) used without explanation. Everyone describes her as gentle, self-effacing and too kind, and the EN must let her be that WITHOUT going saccharine, because the same voice says 「私……死をもって償うしかないの……」 and, later, arranges a man's death.
- SECOND REGISTER, the collapse: short, broken across cells, repetitive, entirely about her own guilt. She never raises her voice anywhere. WRITTEN register, once, and it is what kills her (00000A94:11:110-113): four narration cells with no 「」 — a name, three plain declaratives about quality and quantity, and 「どうかこれをうまく役立てて下さい」. No warmth, no warning, no signature -> Q071.
- HER ONE ARGUMENT, and it is the longest and firmest speech she makes anywhere and she never raises her voice in it (00000EDF:59:144-170): 「全ての命は……／平等です」, other people and animals have families too, a single life being unequal is understandable but nobody deserves to die, and the practical charge 「幸太郎は、美冬ちゃんにドローガを送る以外の方法は考えたの？」, closing on 「命は平等であり不平等。／だったら、できるだけ命を平等にできる方法を考えるべきだった」. She contradicts both her husband and her mother-in-law to their faces.
- HER ABILITY is given FOUR MUTUALLY INCOMPATIBLE conditions by four speakers and the contradiction is used as evidence in-story: dark, narrow places (her own account and her husband's); the same seasonal cherry trigger as 古郡なつみ's (her elder daughter); a clifftop and 四月病 (her husband again, later); and the full account at 00002457:11:9. Cite whichever the file gives and do NOT harmonise -> Q243, Q950. Her own stated conditions: danger only, to people she has met or heard speak, fifteen minutes in a dark place costing her her health, range from ten days to ten seconds. She admits the closed eyes and deep breathing are theatre. She will not say where it came from — 「これは誰にも言ってないの」 — while her own narration calls it 「栄一郎を失った代わりに得られたこの力」.
- Physical: near-blonde brown hair, sharply cut features, pearl-white skin, and BOTH EYES BLUE, NOT ODD — the text says so explicitly against her daughters. Japanese; the settlement's Westerner ancestry. She disliked her own appearance and was bullied for it. Her favourite flower was 沈丁花, not 桜, and its 花言葉 are 不死、不滅、永遠; the narration points out the irony itself -> Q192.
- Narration: she narrates 000010F5 whole in 私, her only narration, and the first 88 cells are a DREAM with the wake-up as the only marker -> Q453. NARRATION VOICE: short, soft, plain past, with the same habit her dialogue has of turning every thought toward another person. She describes her own feelings IN THE NEGATIVE (「ううん、寂しいなんてきれいな気持ちじゃない。／単に、飢えているだけ」) and never once complains. The one sustained piece of reasoning she does alone is faultless.
- SHE SPEAKS POSTHUMOUSLY in at least six modes — a standing apparition, a recording, a walking corpse across two chunks, a voice in a burning settlement, a figure inside an induced dream, and a disembodied voice — AND THE REGISTER IS COMPLETELY UNCHANGED IN EVERY ONE. The text explains why: 「しゃべり方や考え方はこの身体の元持ち主に由来するけど、今意識を支配しているのは女神様だ」. Speech, thought and memory are hers; the will is not. EN MUST ADD NO FLATNESS, NO ECHO AND NO EERINESS ANYWHERE -> Q545, Q496. The narration even flags the living behaviour: she hugs her knees, takes a deep breath and sighs, and 「何から何まで、死人とは思えない行動だ」.
- Her ONE flat line as a corpse is the exception that proves it: 「ありえないことが起こるのが、呪殺事件なんです」, in です・ます, which she never uses to her own daughter.
- THE ONE FILE WHERE HER REGISTER BREAKS is 00002464 and nothing prepares it: the narration says he has never seen her glare and has never heard her disparage anyone, and then 「幸太郎って、頭悪かったかしら？」, 「10年もかけたのに、そんなことで失敗されたらがっかりだわ」. THE GRAMMAR DOES NOT MOVE — the soft ～のよ／～わ／～かしら, the bare 幸太郎 and the opening question about the listener are all intact, and only the content is contemptuous. EN MUST NOT COOL THE VOICE TO CARRY IT -> Q967.
- THE MIMICRY, once (00000E7B:180:108-116): in a burning house she puts on a high, bright voice and gives orders as a woman she watched being knocked out, and scatters twenty armed people with it. Her daughter calls it 「おばあちゃん仕込みのモノマネ」 — the same impersonation habit 新村エリカ has everywhere, and a third character remembers her doing it as a child. It is the only time she is heard doing anything but being gentle in that branch.
- DRUNK, once (00001D17), and it is the only time she is LOUD anywhere: every vowel stretched, 「あっはっはー！」, her mother-in-law called コスプレババア twice to her face. Her daughter's gloss is 「普段はあんなにおしとやかなのに、お酒が入るといつもこうなんだから」. The EN must let the gentle register and this one be the same woman -> Q789.
- THE THING THE TEXT WILL NOT RECONCILE, and translators must not resolve it: she REFUSES the request to the end, in two separate files, with the settlement's own motto turned back on the asker (「だから、2人の子供を殺すの？」) — AND THE WEAPONS ARRIVE ANYWAY, and one file shows her packing them while saying 「美冬ちゃんがこれで人殺しなんてするはずが……」. A character notices the dosing instructions may not be in her hand. Three files, no reconciliation -> Q504, Q740.
- MAJOR LATE RETRACTIONS that change how earlier files must be translated: she NEVER injected herself with ファルシフィカソ — the thing showed her the sight and sound of doing it and her own lifelong honesty made the belief stick, so 000010F5:8:348-357 must be translated as WHAT SHE BELIEVED SHE WAS DOING -> Q460; and the same device is given as the explanation for her elder daughter's confessed murders -> Q461, Q462. She also states that she was the one who turned 新村春花 round (「何とか春花を集落にとどめようとしたけど……。／春花は相変わらず単純で、大して大変じゃなかったわね」) and that she planted the report that predicted its own reader's death -> Q541, Q537.
- Her deaths and last words: 「幸太郎……／ありがとうね。／愛してるわ」 in one branch and 「さようなら幸太郎、愛しているわ」 one cell after arranging his death in another. Those are the ONLY two 愛してる in the project and they belong to the same person. Her own death is shown from three angles and whether she jumped, collapsed or was pushed is left as a simile; who took her head varies by account -> Q168, Q458, Q459.
- Open queries on her: Q009, Q071, Q168, Q173, Q180, Q181, Q192, Q243, Q249, Q453, Q454, Q460, Q461, Q462, Q496, Q504, Q537, Q541, Q545, Q553, Q574, Q740, Q767, Q778, Q789, Q803, Q805, Q817, Q837, Q937, Q938, Q950, Q967.

### 城崎健吾 (きざき けんご, Kengo Kizaki) — 荒田集落, the 城崎 line (smith and toolmaker), ~50, widower
- The CAST block is titled "城崎 and the unnamed 荒田 attackers (00000B27)" because he is first heard as an unseen voice on a telephone; he is identified by another character from the voice alone. The given name 城崎健吾 is in GLOSSARY and is not used in dialogue. About twenty years older than 幸太郎, whom he calls 幸太郎君. As-of: 00002478.
- Pronoun: 俺. Copula だ, です to a very few addressees. Dialect: none. Profanity ceiling: くそ, once or twice, and never in a scene where he is killing.
- HIS BASELINE IS AN EXCLAMATION MARK ON NEARLY EVERY LINE — cheerful, loud, self-deprecating about his own skill (「鉈でハエを切るだけじゃ、腹はふくれないよ！」). AND ITS ABSENCE IS HIS ENTIRE CHARACTERISATION: the notes track it file by file, and in at least six appearances (00000F9F, 000013FD, 00001BA8, 00002057, 00002444 across ~900 cells, and 0000246E across ~180) the exclamation marks are COMPLETELY ABSENT for the whole scene. In his own narration they are absent from every cell while present in his spoken lines — the same split 五島's and 伊勢's narrations have. EN must carry the loudness as a switch, not as a permanent texture.
- EN correlates: contraction rate MEDIUM-HIGH in the loud register, LOW in the quiet one; profanity ceiling ZERO-PLUS-ONE; sentence length SHORT; formality LOW to almost everyone, 大魔女様 upward, and ONE です・ます register with an adult couple he calls 旦那さん and 奥さん.
- THE ATTACKER LAYER, four cells apart from the polite one and that gap IS the horror: shouted タメ口 noun-phrase fragments with no verb of accusation at the victims — 「知花の仇……！」, 「サクラちゃんをよくも……！」, 「呪われた親子め……！　荒田のために死ね……！」 — and flawless 敬語 reporting a completed job to the 大魔女 — 「大魔女様。呪殺事件の犯人を始末できました」. The report line must sound like a subordinate pleased with himself and must NOT sound like a threat. They kill two people without one coarse word.
- HIS SIGNATURE MOVE, done three times across the project: he hands over the means against himself. He presses a SHEATHLESS KNIFE on a schoolgirl and makes carrying it the condition of a ride (00000D11:11:23-40); he puts his knife on the floor and turns his back to a fifteen-year-old who has just called him a murderer — 「もし君が俺を信用できないなら、もう俺は役に立たない。この場で俺の首を刺すといい」; and he invites his own murder in words — 「それとも、今ここで俺を殺すかい？」. He never explains the first one; a third party does.
- HE IS THE SETTLEMENT'S ONLY DISSENTER and it is a reasoning position, not a moral pose: 「犯人なんていない。みんなが怖がって、魔女裁判のように疑わしい人間を殺しているだけ」; the argument that proving a man innocent by watching him REQUIRES a death, so the watch is one man sacrificing others for himself; 「この集落はバカばかりなのか？」; and 「皮肉なことに、この集落の者よりもなつみちゃんたちの方が信用できる」. He is also the one who calls the police and gives the reason the settlement never has — 「国有地だからこそ国に入ってもらわないといけない」.
- AND HE CAVES, repeatedly, and always names his own leverage without excusing it: 「俺がここで武器を振らなければ、俺は村八分になる。／当たり前だろ？」; 「もしみんなに逆らえば、俺はあの子よりも先に自分の作品の錆になるのだ」; 「ごめんよ、許してくれ」; 「家にこもるなんて、卑怯だと思うかい？」 / 「俺は自分で卑怯だと思うけどな……」. EN must let the cowardice be stated flatly and not apologised for.
- HIS BREAKS-OFF ARE TRACKED: at 00000C38:11:33-34 he begins 「でも、大魔女様――」 and substitutes 「えっと、ここの長老が」 in front of an outsider; in later branches the hesitation is GONE and he says 大魔女様 in front of outsiders freely. The presence or absence of the hesitation is branch information -> Q191.
- HIS LAND SPEECH (00000D29:19:76-94) is the only time anyone in the project explains why nobody leaves: railway evictions, territorial disputes between states, indigenous refusal of civilisation, wars of expansion — four examples in four cells, no rhetoric, ending 「死んでも守らないといけない場所」.
- THE ENTRAPMENT (00000D29:19:5-65) is his most complicated scene: he states the 呪殺 mechanism, lets a girl take it as a threat and hold a knife to his throat, then tells her that what she is doing IS 呪殺 — 「今、まさに君がやろうとしていることだ」 — and finally says the whole thing was a lie told so she would feel it from inside. The narrator cannot tell which layer is the lie, and another character later argues the retraction is itself the lie. EN must not tip it -> Q200.
- His wife 城崎百合子 was the first 呪殺 victim, killed the night before she meant to go to the police. He appears as a cheerful widower in five branches and never says how she died; the one account he gives anyone is 14:311-328 of 00002444, and its key line is 「俺は仏でも菩薩でもないし、憎しみがないかと言われたら嘘になる」, with what stops him being her opinion of him. He also names her as the thing that keeps him sane: 「俺は妻が殺されたからこそ、俺は自制心を保てるのだ」 -> Q477.
- Narration: he narrates 0000243F (order 414, 165 lines) in 俺 — his first anywhere in 414 files, and the block carried no narration-voice line before it -> Q932. Narration voice: plain past, short declaratives, one thought per cell, self-accusing WITHOUT self-pity (「情けない話だ。俺はそれ以上、みんなを止める行動を起こせなかった」). He addresses his dead wife in the SECOND PERSON inside his own narration, the only place he does: 「なあ百合子、俺は間違っているよな？」.
- HE IS THE PROJECT'S REFERENCE SOURCE for several things and each must be rendered as fixed material: THE FOUR 荒田 FAMILY LINES — 新村 (head family, martial arts and hunting), 城崎 (weapons and tools), 新島 (farming and the natural world), 瀬 (water and irrigation), with the note that all four have branched so often that a shared surname usually means no close kinship -> Q929; the HUNTING RULE (「母熊を殺したら、小熊も殺せと」); the settlement's ECONOMY (his 鉈 and メイス sell outside as 最高級品 and 「俺がこの荒田の財政を支えている」); the food names made ugly on purpose to keep outsiders away, and 荒田 nearly being called 天田 -> Q773, Q418; and the reading of the 能面/赤装束 rising as a クーデター against 新村本家.
- HE SUSPECTS THE IMPERSONATION, which nobody else in the project does from outside: he asks a man to repeat a conversation they supposedly had the night before, says 「昨夜の君が、本当に君だったのかどうか、確認したい」, and answers 「そんなことをできる人なんて、他に誰が……」 with 「いるじゃないか、1人だけ」 — and never says the name -> Q799.
- Address forms: なつみちゃん, then 君 (which ARRIVES WITH THE KNIFE and stays), 「お姉ちゃん」 once, 藤野さん for an alias he is given, 幸太郎君, 大魔女様, 旦那さん／奥さん.
- Open queries on him: Q173, Q191, Q200, Q418, Q477, Q773, Q799, Q800, Q802, Q929, Q932, Q936, Q971.

### 糸姫 (いとひめ, Ito-hime) — the servant child of 0000107B; LATER the antagonist, 死月妖花
- First appears 0000107B:11:50 as a ten-year-old servant. LATER (as of 000014D7:8:451-461) she is the antagonist and the identity is ASSERTED on screen: 「古郡なつみさん、初めまして。／私は……／糸姫と申します」. This is ONE block, not two -> Q597. As-of: 00002444.
- Pronoun: 私, typical frequency. Copula です. Dialect: none.
- Speech level: 最上級敬語 (申し訳ありません, ～です, ～ます), and SHE DOES NOT DROP IT ONCE IN EIGHT HUNDRED YEARS. She keeps it while feverish; while offering her own body to be eaten; while ordering two corpses about (「殺しはダメだと言いましたよね？」); while announcing she will take every life in a town; while apologising to the women whose families she took; while begging on her face; while being kicked, slapped and stamped on; and while a hunting rifle is fired at her. She NEVER raises her voice and NEVER uses a rough form. She is the only figure in the project whose surface survives eight centuries intact.
- EN correlates: contraction rate ZERO; profanity ceiling ZERO; sentence length VERY SHORT; formality the HIGHEST of any human character; vocabulary a servant child's — as a child she has NO ABSTRACT NOUNS AT ALL, and later ones arrive only where the plot requires them. Tics: none; two stammers, one under fever and one under joy.
- Her two load-bearing early lines: 「皇子様……私、まだ小さいですけど……。／みんなのお腹、いっぱいになりますか？」 — a ten-year-old volunteering to be eaten, in perfect politeness, with NO FEAR WORD — and 「それなら、ここが私の居場所です。／私はいつも皇子様とともにありますから」. EN must let the first be FLAT; the horror is that nobody in the scene is surprised.
- Narration: she narrates 00001505:8:69-521, ~450 cells, her own memory of 800 years earlier, TAKEN OVER IN THE MIDDLE OF A ROW-BLOCK WITH NO MARKER AND NEVER HANDED BACK -> Q605. Narration voice: archaic-polite past, 私, one thought per cell, no irony and no self-defence. IT IS THE ONLY NARRATION IN THE PROJECT WITH NO MODERN NOUN IN IT. It goes almost wordless at the two peaks (an overheard conversation; a fall) and the last sixty cells drop into PRESENT-TENSE FRAGMENTS as she bargains.
- THE BARGAIN, ~67 cells of ONE SIDE of a conversation (00001505:8:455-521) with the other side never printed: it saves her, she gives it human intelligence, it wants corpses to grow from, it puts spores on him so it can find him, it reports that he is dead, she asks it to reach 元木 and revive him and accepts that this will take centuries, it tells her she must kill, she refuses, it threatens to let her go, and she consents -> Q609.
- WHAT SHE WANTS, stated in one cell after two hundred of evasion: 「大切な人のそばにいたいのです」. Everything the project has called 呪殺, 神隠し, 死神 and 死月妖花 is a fifteen-year-old trying to reach a grave.
- She is A LIVING HUMAN and says so — 「いいえ、私は生きた人間です」 — and when detached from the organism she has no strength at all, runs at three people with a syringe and is dodged, and the narration's word for her is 憐れ. She ACCOUNTS for the killings as a quota she could not refuse and then REFUSES THE EXCUSE HERSELF, twice in two branches: 「しかし、殺したことは事実です」.
- The name she gives him, ニイ様, is coined by her on screen out of 新村 and misheard as 兄様; she uses it in every cell of her own narration and in her last words -> Q606. Her own name has the 姫 added as a kindness by a third party five cells after she is called 糸 bare -> Q445, Q419. LATER she gives her name as 「糸」 and not 糸姫 to a stranger in one branch, and the 姫 is restored by a third party from the mountain's etymology -> Q935.
- She is called, before the reveal: 死月妖花, 女神様, ネクロ, アバドン, 彼女. Her own servants use 女神様 exclusively. LATER she states she is NO LONGER THE 核 — someone absorbed about twenty years ago is, called アバドン in a Western language, and she was deliberately not told the identity because she had begun refusing to kill -> Q940.
- SECOND-FRAME WARNING for translators of late files: a translator meeting 000014D7 alone will have no idea that the name is 186 files old, that 糸姫山 — where the scene is set — is named after her, or that the 姫 in it was a kindness.
- Her LAST appearance in the project (00001E41:11:16-28), 85 years later: a drone broadcast turns into her private first person inside one row-block with no marker — a hairpin that broke and is treasured anyway, 「愛しいニイ様」, 「私、幸せです」. The register is her です・ます with the pleading gone and nothing frightening left in it, and she is NOT NAMED anywhere in the file: the かんざし and ニイ様 are the only identification -> Q770.
- Open queries on her: Q419, Q445, Q591, Q597, Q605, Q606, Q609, Q629, Q630, Q633, Q770, Q789, Q935, Q936, Q938, Q940.

### 皇子 (the Prince) — the founder of 荒田集落, narrator of 0000107B
- The Emperor's son, fleeing the western capital in the year before 祀耀元年. As-of: 00001505.
- Pronoun: 私 — NEVER 余, 麿 or any court pronoun, which is itself a statement: he has already stopped being a prince. Typical frequency. Copula だ／である.
- Speech level: plain form, level and unhurried, to everyone INCLUDING the lowest-born servant. He gives instructions rather than orders, and the narration states the reason: 「もはや身分がどうこうと言っている次元ではない」. Particles: ～だろうか (his commonest, and it is ALWAYS a real question); ～のだ (his conclusions); ～ぞ once, to a child.
- TICS: NONE. He is the only major voice in the project with no tic at all. The 「はっはっは！」 that runs in his family for 800 years belongs to his companion, not to him.
- Dialect: none, but the register is deliberately ARCHAIC-PLAIN: 者ども, 女子, 賤しい, 喜ばしい, 叶わず. EN correlates: contraction rate ZERO; profanity ceiling ZERO; sentence length MEDIUM, one clause per line; formality MEDIUM-archaic WITHOUT being stiff; vocabulary court-educated but plain — he explains hunger, cannibalism and geography in the same measured words.
- Narration voice: past tense, 私, and THE FLATTEST IN THE PROJECT after 新村春花's autobiography mode. Two paragraphs of third-person chronicle open the file and hand over to him with no marker. He states that everyone alive has eaten human flesh and that those who refused were eaten, in two sentences, with no self-defence and no horror word. EN MUST ADD NEITHER.
- What he decides, and each is a fixed piece of lore: to be dead, so the eastern capital is not burdened; to name the valley 荒田 rather than 天田 so that nothing attractive draws attention -> Q418; to give up the title and take another name (the closing narration says the man who abandoned his rank called himself 新村 and never says it is him — he says it in his own mouth 10 years later at 00001505:8:76-77) -> Q445; and HE COINS THE SETTLEMENT MOTTO, 「1人はみんなのために、みんなは1人のために」, in its origin scene, 800 years before every other use of it -> Q420, Q174.
- LATER (00001505) he is shown as a man rather than a founder and the register is unchanged. He asks a ten-year-old servant what she would like to call him — the only thing he is ever asked for -> Q606. HIS ONE ADMISSION is dragged out of him and broken across three cells, 「もし……／こんな境遇でなければ……／私だって糸姫を……」, never finished and never said to her. THE PROMISE is the hinge of the whole project: within ten years the age will end, and 「私はまたここへ……／糸を迎えに来る」, with a hairpin he carved himself — and he drops the 姫 for the only time -> Q607.
- The file will not let him be innocent: 糸姫's word for him, four times over alone on a road, is 卑怯者, for leaving a decision with her and taking none himself.
- Open queries: Q014, Q417, Q418, Q419, Q420, Q421, Q445, Q606, Q607.

### 新村桔梗 (にいむら ききょう, Kikyo Niimura) — 大魔女 two generations back, dead
- First a SIGNATURE ON A DOCUMENT (00001355:8:45-48), LATER the narrator of TWENTY-FOUR CONSECUTIVE FILES (orders 366-389, 0000212D through 000021C9) — THE LONGEST SINGLE-NARRATOR RUN IN THE PROJECT, covering her whole life from 16 to death. As-of: 000021C9.
- Pronoun: 私, LESS often than typical — her documents are almost entirely subjectless; 我々 for the settlement inside the translated text she presents. Copula です in the note, だ in the chronicle.
- 敬語 UPWARD IS ABSOLUTE and she NEVER uses タメ口 to anyone on screen: です・ます to her father with 父上, to her husband with 晃様 and then 晃さん, to her mother-in-law with お義母さん, to an outsider with 篠崎さん.
- HER TWO WRITTEN REGISTERS, four cells apart, and they must stay apart in EN: (1) the COVERING NOTE, 丁寧 to an unknown descendant — 「少しでも子孫たちが読みやすいよう、今の言葉に翻訳しています。どうか、これを役立ててください」; (2) the DOCUMENT she is presenting, plain declarative 祀耀482 chronicle that HARDENS INTO CLASSICAL IMPERATIVE at the end — 「未来の荒田の者たちよ、子孫たちよ。／これから述べることをしかと後世へ伝えよ」, with ～べし. How much of the chronicle is hers and how much is the original is never marked -> Q534.
- EN correlates: contraction rate ZERO; profanity ceiling ZERO; sentence length MEDIUM in the note, SHORT in the chronicle, LONG in her narration; formality HIGH throughout; vocabulary religious and administrative (降臨, 転生, 分身, いけにえ, 撃退).
- NARRATION VOICE: formal, essayistic, REASONING-FIRST. Long sentences that set out a principle and then apply it, rhetorical questions aimed at the reader, and an almost total absence of self-pity. She states her feelings only AFTER working out what they are (「これは――／後悔だ」) and corrects herself out loud (「何を考えているの、私」). THE RUN OPENS WITH NO FIRST PERSON AT ALL — an essay addressed to the reader defining 才媛 — and the switch into 私 is unmarked; she is not named until 8:16, by her father -> Q869, Q108.
- HER RUNNING JOKE, the only frivolous thing she does anywhere: 「私、／ものっっっっすごく／暇なんで！」 split across three cells, used four times across four files WITH A DIFFERENT NUMBER OF っ EACH TIME. It is how she breaks a silence and the only thing her husband learns to anticipate. EN must reproduce the varying elongation -> Q842.
- HER METHOD, stated once and applied for sixty years: 「あらゆるものには因果律が存在する。……そこには必ず理屈があり、原因があり、結果がある」. She is the FIRST person in the project to refuse a supernatural explanation and the only one who is right. Note the family resemblance to 五島絵梨奈's credo — fix distinct EN wordings for the two.
- Her second principle, given before she has any evidence: a 戒め set up to survive a crisis decays into 伝承 and 神話 once the crisis passes, so the rule survives and the reason does not. EVERY 大魔女 AFTER HER IS AN INSTANCE OF IT. Her defence of the 掟, given while objecting to infanticide: 「掟とは人を縛るためのものではない。／人を守るために掟があるのだ」 — EN must let her hold both positions at once.
- SHE LIES, calmly and twice, and narrates both: 「私も嘘をついた」 one cell after lying to an outsider, and a deliberate lie to the settlement about why the ban exists, on the ground that a ban WITH a reason has been forgotten four times already. She also CATCHES A LIE BY EAR — a man says he has never been to 飯沢県 and his accent is 飯沢県's, which she can hear because she was born in 東京. HIS LINES ARE NOT WRITTEN IN DIALECT and EN must not invent one -> Q843.
- WHAT SHE BUILDS is the machinery every modern file runs on: the ドローガ ban; the rewritten 法要 that makes ファルシフィカソ a ritual implement so the antidote is manufactured forever; the ヴェルジ mummy sealed behind a 封石; and the translated 蔵書 that a modern character reads 65 years later -> Q865, Q864. SHE ALSO GIVES THE RECIPE AWAY, which is how the modern sect has it -> Q450, Q452.
- HER MEMORY FAILS ON SCREEN AND SHE NARRATES IT: 35 lines of an old woman who knows she is waiting for something and cannot remember what, in front of a mummy whose story she can no longer finish (「それは……／確か……」). Her own later reading is that the thing could not move while she suspected it, so the forgetting is not idle -> Q828.
- HER TWO-DATE SIGNATURE: the same covering note printed twice, signed 「祀耀735年　大魔女　新村桔梗」 in one block and 「1942年　大魔女　新村桔梗」 in another. The project's ONLY real-calendar year -> Q827 (owner-blocking).
- Her last passage is an address to descendants she will never meet, the same audience as her covering note: the clues are in the 蔵書, she never wrote the 仕上げ, 「どうか死月妖花を滅ぼしてくれ」, and twice on the way out 「私の考えは――／甘かった」.
- Open queries: Q108, Q450, Q452, Q534, Q536, Q558, Q827 (owner-blocking), Q828, Q842, Q843, Q864, Q865, Q869.

### 藤吉郎 (とうきちろう, Tokichiro) — 麻生田家's 台所奉行, narrator of 00001092 and 000010AA
- A period narrator ~340 years before the story present. As-of: 000010AA.
- Pronoun: オレ IN KATAKANA, in narration and dialogue, and the FIRST オレ narrator anywhere (古郡良治 is 俺). He states it MORE often than typical — it opens dozens of cells and is the engine of his self-assertion (「オレは台所奉行だ！」, 「オレが天下を治めるのだ」). DO NOT DELETE THE "I" IN EN.
- TWO registers by addressee: to a friend, loud タメ口 with a MIRRORED GREETING (「よう吉郎！　久しぶりだなあ！」 repeated back word for word); to anyone above him, self-lowering 敬語 with archaic humility formulas that ALWAYS TRAIL — 「ありがたきことで……」, 「もったいなきことで……」 — and both hands on the floor.
- Particles: ～だ／～のだ (declarative and often triumphant); ～ぞ; ～か／～のか (self-questioning); ～で (the trailing humility formulas). Copula だ. Tics: 「ガハハと笑いあう」; 出世 used eleven times as the thing he wants; the repeated frame 「オレにできることは何だ！？」.
- EN correlates: contraction rate MEDIUM-HIGH; profanity ceiling ZERO — he never swears once in 500 cells; sentence length SHORT and driven, question-heavy when planning; formality LOW to equals and archaic-HIGH upward; vocabulary practical and administrative (経費, 節約, 分国法, 一円知行, 年貢, 兵力) and it RISES when he reasons. Register is period-plain with peasant edges.
- Narration voice: past tense, オレ, energetic and entirely WITHOUT self-pity or irony. He explains his own job's boundaries pedantically, reasons in numbered consequences, and STAGES TWO INTERNAL VOICES AGAINST EACH OTHER — 「こんなことをしても無駄だと言うオレ。／こうしなければ生き延びられないと言うオレ」. The same two-voices device as 五島's dream narration and 新村夏菜's deliberation, and no file nods at another.
- Three name forms in two files — 吉郎, 藤吉郎, 藤殿 -> Q443. 00001092:11:186 is 「オレの記憶は、ここで終わっている」, a survivor's present perfect that never says when or to whom he is telling this. 000010AA:11:245 calls the drug 「何とかと言う薬」 eleven cells after using its name -> Q444.
- Open queries: Q421, Q423, Q443, Q444.

### 内府 (the Daifu) — narrator of 000010C3, 祀耀340
- A senior court administrator; never given a personal name, and neither is his lord — both are offices throughout -> Q421. As-of: 000010C3.
- Pronoun: 私, typical frequency. Copula だ／である in narration.
- Speech level: 最上級敬語 upward and NOTHING ELSE on screen — he speaks to exactly one person in the whole file (「これはこれは殿下、ご機嫌うるわしゅう」, 「いかほどご入り用で……？」, 「はい、心得ております」, 「はい、有り難き幸せ」) — and FLAT IMPERATIVES with no politeness at all to his hired men. Particles: ～のだ／～のである (conclusions); ～で……？ (his careful questions, ALWAYS trailing); ～だろうか (private reasoning). Tics: none. Register: court-formal archaic (薨去, 範疇, 大義, 些細, 茫然).
- EN correlates: contraction rate ZERO; profanity ceiling ZERO; sentence length MEDIUM to LONG in narration and VERY SHORT in speech; formality the HIGHEST in the project outside the menu navigator; vocabulary political and military with a strong analytical streak — he reconstructs a whole drug history from two sentences and a memory.
- Narration voice: past tense, 私, COLD, ORDERLY AND ENTIRELY WITHOUT SELF-JUSTIFICATION. He orders ten men killed, watches them sing, prices a party at 39億円 and describes a man walking on his hands, all in the same measured clauses. His only statement of feeling is 「こんな恐ろしいところ、少しでも早く去ってしまいたい」. EN MUST NOT WARM HIM AND MUST NOT MAKE HIM A VILLAIN; he is an administrator.
- His one moral sentence is QUOTED FROM SOMEONE ELSE — 「今10人死ぬか、将来10万人死ぬか、どちらを選ぶか」 — and he neither endorses nor rejects it. His closing instruction is the chunk's last line and the project's oldest surviving document: 「だが、後世に伝えなくてはならない。／ドローガには――／決して手を出すな。」 — THE SAME SENTENCE a dying man gave him earlier in the file, IN THE OPPOSITE REGISTER. BOTH MUST LAND -> Q425.
- 11:235 「（現代の金額換算で39億円）」 is a MODERN PARENTHETICAL inside his narration with no frame -> Q424. 11:287 is the project's title word said in-world for the first time and he does not explain it -> Q426.
- Open queries: Q421, Q424, Q425, Q426.

### 篠崎ハジメ (しのざき ハジメ, Hajime Shinozaki) — the man who takes the 荒田 cherries, 祀耀729
- On screen and speaking at 000021A5:8:5; LATER narrates a whole file (000021CF, order 390, 489 lines) in 私, picking up the frame at a file boundary with NO MARKER, and is not named in it until somebody else says 「篠崎君」 at 8:58 -> Q919. As-of: 000021CF.
- Pronoun: 私, typical frequency. Copula です.
- Speech level: MAXIMAL 敬語 WITH MILITARY EDGES — he salutes before speaking, uses 参上しました and 恐れ入ります, and SHOUTS his self-introduction. Particles ～ます／～ません, ～ましょう, and the clipped official ～いたく (「いただきたく」). Mid-thirties at the first visit and old at the second.
- Tics: NONE — the register is the whole surface. Under pressure the only thing that moves is THE VOLUME: 「ま、まさか！　そんなつもりは毛頭ありません！」, 「ほ、本当ですか！？」.
- DIALECT PROBLEM, and it has no obvious solution: he is from 飯沢県 and HIS LINES ARE NOT WRITTEN IN DIALECT. Another character hears it and the reader cannot — 「なぜなら、訛りが飯沢県の話し方だからだ」 — and the detection is what exposes his lie -> Q843.
- EN correlates: contraction rate NONE; profanity ceiling ZERO; sentence length SHORT and clipped; formality VERY HIGH; vocabulary bureaucratic-military (派遣, 参上, 提供, 伐採から運搬まで).
- NARRATION VOICE: measured, self-accusing, administrative. Short declaratives; he reasons in numbered steps and states his own guilt as FACT without decorating it — 「実験体を志願した同胞たちは、モノ扱いだった」, 「私の手によって多くの人々が命を落とした」. He interrupts his own moral reckoning with body complaints (「しかし、寒いのはいやだ。／早く帰ろう」), which is the only humour he has.
- SECOND IDENTITY: the sect changes his 戸籍 and he becomes 『金崎 勉』, a 飯沢県 official responsible for the 荒田 district, which he asks for himself. Both names carry a groundless 地上げ rumour that is the reason 荒田 closes further.
- He is the ONLY outsider in the project who keeps a promise to 荒田: asked for results in writing, he brings them, late, in person, and apologises for the delay. His last fifteen years are conspicuous goodness and the narration names the motive — 「せめてもの罪滅ぼし」. He is stabbed in an alley by a stranger on his grandson's birthday, works out ON THE WAY DOWN who sent him, and his last regret is that the report was never handed over in person.
- Open queries: Q450, Q451, Q843, Q918, Q919.

### 後白河糸織 (ごしらかわ いとおり, Itoori Goshirakawa) — the transfer student, 00001533
- The project's ONLY character with a sustained dialect, and an owner-blocking decision hangs on her -> Q611. First seen at a distance at 00001533:8:74; first line is her self-introduction at 8:171. As-of: 00001533.
- Pronoun: 私, typical frequency. Copula や.
- Speech level: 関西弁 (Osaka) タメ口 to EVERYONE including the teacher and a stranger she has known one minute. No 敬語 anywhere except the formula 「よろしくお願いします」. Particles ～やねん, ～やな／～やなあ, ～んよな, ～へん／～ひん, ～かもな, ～たってな. Tics: 「あははは！」 at her own expense; 「ほな」 as a goodbye; 「ほんま」 as an intensifier; 「せやなあ」 as agreement; 「ええ」 for いい throughout.
- EN correlates: contraction rate HIGH; profanity ceiling ZERO; sentence length SHORT and fast; formality ZERO; vocabulary plain and physical. HER ENGLISH MUST NOT BE A BRITISH OR AMERICAN REGIONAL DIALECT — the decision is owner-blocking -> Q611.
- She is PHYSICALLY GENTLE about the thing she cannot know: she crushes a hand that turns out to be bandaged, apologises, strokes the dressing and says 「この手、すごく大変なことがあったみたいやな。／大丈夫や、すぐによくなる」. The narrator's word for her face is 慈しみ, which is the word she reaches for about 糸姫.
- SHE HAS NO MEMORY before a specific date the previous week — not how she came to the town, not how she lived, only her name — and she says so cheerfully. SHE OFFERS TO BE THE DEAD GIRL, unprompted, having been told only that somebody who resembles her is dead: 「ほなら、私、その子の生まれ変わりでええよ」 and 「今の私は、誰でもないわけやし」.
- SECOND-FRAME NOTE: this is a SEPARATE block from 糸姫, not an update to it, because the whole function of the character is that NOBODY IN THE TEXT KNOWS WHO SHE IS. The file never asserts the identity and the narrator explicitly refuses to decide — 「彼女が糸姫の生まれ変わりなのかどうか定かではない」. EN MUST LEAVE BOTH READINGS ALIVE IN EVERY LINE -> Q630, Q614.

### the 核 / 女神様 (00002444:32) — the black mass at the top of 糸姫山
- The present core of the organism; 糸姫 is the FORMER core and says so on screen. Never identified. A separate block from 糸姫; cross-reference if a later file names her -> Q940, Q597. As-of: 00002444.
- Pronoun: 私. Copula: none observed. Speech level: NONE — no 敬語 and no recognisable adult タメ口. Two- and three-word fragments, most of them nouns with the verb missing, and almost no particles. The narration's own reading: 「声色とかではなく、話し方のぎこちなさが、妙に子供っぽかった」. She is an adult woman of about 春花's age who has apparently never been taught to speak.
- Particles: ～の; ～よお stretched, in the ONE line she raises, 「ママに会いたいよお……」. Tics: none, and she has NO LAUGH. NINE OF HER TURNS ARE PRINTED AS A CELL CONTAINING 「　　　　　　　　」 AND NOTHING ELSE — eight full-width spaces inside quote marks — while the other speaker keeps talking into them. This is a typographic device, not dialogue -> Q939.
- EN correlates: contraction rate ZERO; profanity ceiling ZERO; sentence length VERY SHORT and OFTEN UNGRAMMATICAL; formality NONE; vocabulary PRE-SCHOOL — ママ, パパ, 会いたい, 嬉しい, 名前, and not one abstract noun. Her one polite form is 「ありがとう」.
- She does not know her own name, her father's name or her mother's name. Her entire negotiating position is 「あの子の……代わり……」, and offered a search for her mother instead she accepts at once. SHE THANKS 春花 FOR THE PROMISE ALONE, before anything is done, and 春花's narration reads it as somebody who has never learned to doubt anyone. AND THEN SHE WITHDRAWS IT in four cells with no explanation — 「もういい。しなくていい。／私からあなたへのお願いは1つだけ／できるだけ長生きしてね？」 — and the last block reveals it is a SENTENCE rather than a kindness -> Q940.
- 「私……とても嬉しい」 / 「ママを……／取り返せるから」 is the one thing she says that cannot be parsed, and the file never explains it. 取り返す, NOT 会う — keep the verb.

### Second-frame blocks: doubles, masks, impersonations and hallucinations
These exist as separate CAST blocks so that a translator does not leak a reveal. In every case the rule is the same: the EN must be INDISTINGUISHABLE from whatever it is pretending to be, and must not hedge, italicise, cool or make eerie.
- **the 死神 (000004F9, 0000050E, 00000564)** — the masked figure. SPEECH: NONE, across three files and two fights. It does not react to being called お父さん or 死神. Its silence must stay ABSOLUTE in EN — no grunts, no breathing, no added stage noise. THREE branch outcomes for the identity (新村美冬; never unmasked; 古郡茜) and NONE may be foreshadowed in the earlier files -> Q119. The narration calls it 「死神」 IN QUOTE MARKS and 「怪人」 without them — keep the quote marks where the JP has them; they are a narrator refusing to accept the word. Origin of the costume is given in full at 000008A2:8:24-95 and is fixed lore: knives sewn inside the 赤装束, 鉈 and メイス as 荒田's equivalent of a sword, a drug that unlocks the muscles for about ten minutes, and the 能面 so that fighters cannot be identified — in 荒田's own vocabulary 魔女 means what would now be called a priest and 死神 means literally the dead.
- **the 大魔女 voice (00000779)** — 新村春花 impersonating her grandmother for 70 cells. 丁寧 carrying flat imperatives; THE POLITENESS IS THE THREAT and nothing in it ever rises. She is THE ONLY CHARACTER IN THE PROJECT WHO FINISHES EVERY SENTENCE. Not one marker of 春花's own register may leak — no ～ぞ, no ～だろ, no くそ, no contractions — and the drop back to her boyish voice inside one cell at 8:102 is the punchline -> Q109. Note: the REAL 大魔女 never appears in that file, so do NOT let this block define her.
- **白般若 (00000F24)** — 新村美冬 under the mask, dead two years, SILENT for roughly five hundred cells. It is addressed, taunted, kicked, shoved and thanked and answers nothing; its only communications are physical. THE NARRATION ABOUT IT USES 「やつ」「こいつ」「白般若」 AND NOTHING ELSE AND IT IS NEVER GENDERED — EN MUST STAY GENDERLESS from 64:136 to 142:138 and must not reach for "he" or "she", which is hard because the taunts aimed at it read as male-coded in English -> Q379. The narration keeps guessing wrong about it out loud; let the wrong guesses stand.
- **the eyeless woman (00000EDF)** — 五島絵梨奈's double, with her eyes cut out. Soft feminine plain form, LEVEL AND UNHURRIED, never raised once in five appearances; she gives instructions as invitations and the narration calls her voice at its worst 「今までで1番優しい耽美な声」. DO NOT MAKE HER HISS AND DO NOT MAKE HER GRAND — she sounds like somebody being kind. Fixed repetitions: 「サード・ジェネレーション」 as her only address form (~14 times), 「あなたを助けられるのは私だけ」 and 「私はあなたの味方」 repeated verbatim in four places and once out of a DIFFERENT mouth, and 「痛いのは最初だけ」. HER IDENTITY IS NEVER ASSERTED: the same two catchphrases come out of a voice the narrator finally names as 「古郡先輩……？」, WITH A QUESTION MARK. EN must not settle it in either direction -> Q288.
- **the imagined 新村春花 (00000EF6)** — 古郡なつみ's hallucination. IDENTICAL to the real character in every marker: 私, ～ぞ, ～だろ, お前, 「バカかよ……」, and her profanity ceiling holds even inside somebody else's head. TWO MODES: a signposted dream, and a ~three-block HALLUCINATION THAT IS NOT SIGNPOSTED ANYWHERE. THE EN MUST BE INDISTINGUISHABLE FROM THE REAL CHARACTER'S EN — no hedging, no italics, no dream diction — because the reveal depends on the reader having believed it. Sixteen cells are replayed VERBATIM in a later block to prove it and both printings must be WORD-IDENTICAL in EN -> Q318, Q319, Q333.
- **the 天秤 (00001184:24:2-22)** — the scales 新村茅萱 says she carries. AN OBJECT, NOT A PERSON: 僕 for itself, 君 for her, and the pronouns must NOT gender or personify it in EN. Plain タメ口, needling, three turns, all questions, plain and juvenile vocabulary deliberately at odds with what it is asking about. It argues her out of her own confession and the block ends on her verdict on her own charge sheet — 「その罪状とは――／空。」, one word in a cell of its own. EN must keep it an object with a voice and must NOT make it a devil, a conscience or an inner child by word choice -> Q463.
- **the 「あっちの方にいるよ」 voice (0000110D, 00001184)** — untagged and unattributed; do NOT join it to the 天秤 -> Q456.
- **モトキザクラ様 and A子 (000009FE)**, **the 街角の占い師 and 204号室の女 (00000A18)** — figures inside 怪談 performed by 古郡なつみ and 新村茅萱 respectively; they belong to the storyteller's register, not to the world.
- **大翔 (00001325, 00001B98 area)** — the boy in 新村夏菜's exchange diary, who does not exist; both handwritings are hers.
- **ヴェルジ／トミ (00000F24)** — the girl in the book, dead about 450 years, fourteen. Plain form, no politeness of any kind, bare declaratives, several with no verb (「もう、ダメ」 has no copula at all). No tics; what she has instead is REPETITION. THE NESTING is four layers deep — a narrator, a girl reading aloud in です・ます, the book's old-style prose, and 『』 around the speaker — and the engine sets the whole reading as 「」 cells attributed to the reader. HER QUOTED SPEECH IS NOT ARCHAIC; only the frame around it is. EN must keep the girl audibly YOUNGER AND PLAINER than the reading voice around her, and must NOT make her tragic on the page — the horror is that she reports her own symptoms like a fact and then acts on them -> Q385, Q387.

---

## 2. Minor speakers

One line each: name, pronoun, speech level, one distinguishing feature, as-of. Blocks already covered in
section 1 (principals and second-frame blocks) are not repeated here. Where a line says "「――」 channel",
see GLOBAL-B: the engine uses a bare 「――」 prefix for the non-focus speaker in a two-voice scene (-> Q047).

**Family, school and 元木町 regulars**
- 五島's mother — no pronoun observed; warm-maternal タメ口, question-heavy (three questions in a row, never one); she asks 「あら」 and stretches every vowel when drunk, and her register does not adjust by one particle when talking to a daughter who cannot answer. As-of 0000210E.
- 五島's father — no pronoun; タメ口 shouted through a mock-polite opener, 「いいか絵梨奈！」 command frame, refers to himself as 「おとーさん」 in a sing-song. A cheerful drunk, not a menacing one. One appearance. As-of 000002F8.
- 向井 (Mukai) — homeroom teacher of 2年2組; no pronoun; 丁寧 school-notice register, and his spoken register (first line in 200 files, at 00001533) is identical to his email register. No contractions. As-of 00001533.
- 谷崎 (Tanizaki) — homeroom teacher of 3年1組; no pronoun; 丁寧 school-notice register BUT BROKEN — he writes 「とのことだす」 for 「とのことです」, doubles 詳しく in one sentence, and signs off a cancellation caused by a murder with 「ではみなさん、よい休日を！」. The errors are the character. As-of 00001A6B.
- unnamed man at the door (00000024:51) — no pronoun; casual-warm ～だね; 春花 addresses him as おとうさん. As-of 00000024.
- 市村 (Ichimura) — 五島絵梨奈's primary-school homeroom teacher, always 市村先生; 私; teacherly 丁寧 over feminine タメ口 (～わね, ～なさい, ～のよ), fully です・ます to the class. As-of 00001704.
- unnamed 小学校の担任 and the class (00001B98) — 先生 as a name-as-role pronoun (「先生は旦那と山菜取りに行って」); 丁寧 to children, feminine, ～ですねえ／～でしょ; a married woman who volunteers her own aches. As-of 00001B98.
- unnamed 新村春花のクラスメイト2人 — 私; ordinary schoolgirl タメ口, friendly and a little teasing; one speaks in 「」 and one is prefixed 「――」. As-of 00001A5C.
- unnamed 2年2組のクラスメイト — collective; one 丁寧 formula in chorus and then two bare タメ口 reactions. As-of 00001533.
- unnamed 小学校のクラスメイト (00001704) — 俺 in one line; children's タメ口, warm and loud. As-of 00001704.
- unnamed 生徒会長 (0000207E) — 元木高校 student council president, a new second-year; 私; bright です・ます with exclamation marks that DOES NOT STOP when the subject turns awkward. As-of 0000207E.
- unnamed シャベッターを見る2人の元木高校生 (000017CB) — no pronoun; schoolgirl タメ口. As-of 000017CB.
- unnamed high-school bystanders (000003A8) — 俺; 乱暴, shouted, no 敬語 at all. As-of 000003A8.
- unnamed ザッハ high-school customers (00000564) — collective gossip; タメ口 whispering, ～だって (hearsay), 「マジで！？」. As-of 00000564.
- 匿名 — the anonymous posters on 元木高校掲示板; 俺 AND 私 mixed, and KEEP THE MIX: it signals mixed-gender anonymity; 乱暴/net-slang with no punctuation discipline. As-of 00001A65.
- unnamed 女ケ沢市 bullies (00000682) — two collectives four years apart; お前 for the target and 俺 once; 乱暴 タメ口, shouted, no 敬語. As-of 00000682.
- unnamed バレー部 coach (00000617) — no pronoun; 乱暴 coaching タメ口, shouted; barks names and orders only. As-of 00001849.
- unnamed バレー部 teammates (00000617) — collective; タメ口, muttered, three lines total. As-of 00000617.
- unnamed 外崎 grandparents (0000088B:35) — 古郡茜's parents; the grandmother is warm 丁寧-casual with a smile in every line; THE GRANDFATHER SPEAKS A DIALECT THE NARRATOR STATES OUTRIGHT HE CANNOT UNDERSTAND. As-of 0000200D.
- 金井桂子 (Keiko Kanai) — 新村美冬's grandmother; no pronoun; plain and warm to a reporter, no 敬語 and no hedge — 「この日本に花を咲かせてくれる神様に違いない」, stated as fact. As-of 0000170A.
- unnamed 弔問客 (00001A97) — mourners at a funeral, collective, the 「――」 voices; 私; adult タメ口, murmured, feminine in most lines. As-of 00001A97.

**Police, officials and institutions**
- unnamed 元木警察署 colleagues (000003B4, 000003BC) — mixed-sex collective, 俺 AND 私; タメ口 among themselves and INSTANT 敬語 the moment 伊勢 appears. As-of 0000242E.
- unnamed young officer at 元木警察署 (00002450:18) — 伊勢's night-duty partner; 僕 (not 自分, which makes him sound younger than the police collective); です・ます to 伊勢 without one lapse across 217 cells; 元木町-born. As-of 00002450.
- unnamed detective at the hospital (0000037D) — described as 「おじいさんのような刑事」; no pronoun; 丁寧 but folksy, soft openers before hard questions. As-of 0000037D.
- unnamed 佐波県警の私服警官 (00000F0D) — おっちゃん AS A PRONOUN FOR HIMSELF, never 俺 or 私; over-familiar タメ口 to a child he has just met, pitched deliberately to sound like a man on the make, and IT DOES NOT CHANGE ONE WORD when he turns out to be on duty. As-of 00000F0D.
- unnamed 聞き込みの警察官2人 (00001FE9) — the senior uses 僕 to a child, plain タメ口 to his junior and softened 丁寧 to householders; the junior uses です・ます and EVERY ONE OF HIS LINES IS PREFIXED 「――」. As-of 00001FE9.
- unnamed 西佐波警察 radio operator (000004F9) — no pronoun; 丁寧 radio procedure (了解しました, どうぞ, 見込んでください). As-of 000004F9.
- unnamed 捜索ヘリの搭乗員 and ground control (00000E7B) — no pronoun; 丁寧 radio procedure from the airborne side and PLAIN-FORM ORDERS from the ground side, the ground side marked only by a 「――」 prefix with no 「」. As-of 00000E7B.
- unnamed 刑務官たち (0000156A) — two lines only, and the gap between them is the institution: a barked 「204番、移動！」 to a prisoner and a 丁寧 rebuke to a colleague, 「山垣さん、そこまでになさい」. As-of 0000156A.
- 山垣 (Yamagaki) — the young prison officer on the transfer bus; 私; です・ます TO A CONVICTED PRISONER, which is the whole of the character; she answers a question she is not supposed to, looks round the bus first, and is shut down mid-sentence. As-of 0000156A.
- unnamed 元木刑務所の受付 (000020A2) — no pronoun; です・ます service-desk, PREFIXED 「――」 THROUGHOUT -> Q047. As-of 000020A2.
- unnamed 拘置所の係員 (00002478) — TWO LAYERS BY ADDRESSEE that must stay apart: flat shouted タメ口 with a bare surname to the detainee (「やめろ新村！　離れろ！」) and unbroken です・ます with no softening and no apology to the visitor (「あと5分です」). As-of 00002478.
- unnamed police-station receptionist (00000488:34) — no pronoun; casual-kind タメ口 to a child (～てね), 丁寧 on the internal phone. As-of 00000488.
- 中嶋長司 (Choji Nakajima) — 官房長官, formerly mayor of 元木町; NO PRONOUN AT ALL — he speaks entirely in institutional plurals (「政府でも」, 「我々の方にも」); 丁寧 official-podium register with passive constructions, hedges and numbers given without sources. As-of 00002096.
- unnamed 記者たち (0000156A) — collective; 丁寧 but shouted; every line is a question and several are accusations. As-of 0000156A.
- unnamed 特殊部隊の2人 (000020BB) — the leader is 俺 with 乱暴-edged タメ口; the young man is 僕 with です・ます and ～っす, and HIS LINES ARE THE 「――」 CHANNEL throughout (「僕、今月末、結婚するんです……」) -> Q835, Q047. As-of 000020BB.
- unnamed 病院スタッフ2人 (00002078) — 私たち once; both タメ口, colleagues of the same rank, one in 「」 and one prefixed 「――」 -> Q836, Q047. As-of 00002078.
- unnamed hospital doctor (00000EF6) — 我々 once; です・ます throughout and THE FILE'S MOST CAREFULLY MANAGED VOICE — he hedges before bad news with ellipses and abandoned sentences and drops the hedges entirely for good news. As-of 00000EF6.
- unnamed hospital nurses (00000EF6) — collective; 丁寧 to patients, warm and brisk, WITH THE POLITENESS BURNED OFF ENTIRELY BY SURPRISE. As-of 00000EF6.
- unnamed 女医 (00001A52, 00001A6B) — the clinic doctor; 私; warm feminine 丁寧 over タメ口 (～かしら, ～わね, ～のよね？) with a doctor's imperatives. As-of 00002066.
- unnamed doctor (00000801, 00000818) — no pronoun; です・ます, HEDGED WITH PAUSES WHEN THE NEWS IS BAD AND UNHEDGED WHEN IT IS GOOD. As-of 00000818.
- unnamed 医師 (000021FE:11:0) — no pronoun; です・ます, the clinical formula. One line. As-of 000021FE.
- unnamed 園長先生 (00000F0D) — head of a children's home; no pronoun; warm 丁寧-casual to a child in her care; two lines, both encouragement. As-of 00000F0D.
- unnamed 元木大学の指導教員 (0000209C, 000020AE) — 私; plain タメ口 downward with occasional 丁寧, the register of an academic supervisor who likes the student. As-of 000020AE.

- unnamed 元木成金 madams (000005C1) — 私; the stereotyped wealthy-madam register, ～ざます／～ざますわ alternating with ～ですわ／～ますわ. A CLASS MARKER, NOT A REGIONAL ACCENT, and the register carries content a listener calls 「かなり下品」. As-of 000005C1.

- unnamed 女ケ沢動物園の私服巡回員 (00001755) — 僕; careful 丁寧-タメ口 aimed at a small child; stammers when accused (「ちち違う！」). As-of 00001755.

THE CONVERTED RESIDENTS OF THE STORY PRESENT HAVE NO LINES AT ALL — do not give them a sound. A third, modern, masked group speaks an ARCHAIC FLAT register (～のだ, ～てはならない, お客人) that matches no other modern voice and BREAKS EXACTLY ONCE, IN FEAR -> Q557; they are corpses in stolen costume, not residents -> Q581.
