- お姉ちゃん -> Sis (as of 0000095B, from 夏菜) · お姉さん -> Sis (as of 00000A18)
- NOTE: 茅萱 is also the common noun for chigaya grass (00000BB7:11:160). A grave-offering scene needs BOTH senses at once -> Q193.

**新村夏菜 — Kana Niimura** (にいむら かな)
- 夏菜 (bare) -> Kana (as of 0000095B) · 夏菜さん -> Kana-san (as of 000014EE) · 夏菜ちゃん -> Kana-chan (as of 00000A48)
- カナカナ -> Kanakana (as of 0000243F) · か……な…… -> Ka... na... (as of 0000151C, broken across cells)
- **ALIAS: 古村秋菜 -> Akina Furumura** (as of 00000F0D:8:131). Built from her own name by 新->古 and 夏->秋 and explained ON SCREEN at 8:133. Western name order makes the substitution invisible -> **Q353 (owner)**. 秋菜 -> Akina (呼び捨て, 44:96) · 秋菜ちゃん -> Akina-chan (38:156).
- ニームラ副団長 / 副団長ニームラ -> Vice-Captain Niimura (as of 00000EDF:192:6; she corrects anyone who uses her given name while the game is on)
- 天才ハンター少女 -> genius hunter girl (as of 00001B76:11:22; 新村エリカ's tag, a variant of the 天才少女 family)

**新村幸太郎 — Kotaro Niimura** (にいむら こうたろう)
- 幸太郎 (bare) -> Kotaro (as of 00000A7C) · 幸太郎さん -> Kotaro-san (as of 0000095B) · 幸太郎君 -> Kotaro-kun (as of 00001154)
- 幸太郎伯父さん -> Uncle Kotaro (as of 0000095B)
- 新村 (bare surname) -> Niimura (as of 00002478)
- お父さん -> Dad (as of 00000B9F) · パパ -> Papa (as of 0000095B)
- 兄貴 -> big brother (as of 00000E31; from his twin 新村栄一郎)

**新村サクラ — Sakura Niimura** (にいむら サクラ; KATAKANA given name in the source — the katakana is not a reading aid, it is how the name is written)
- サクラ (bare) -> Sakura (as of 00000A7C) · サクラさん -> Sakura-san
- お母さん -> Mom (as of 000010F5) · ママ -> Mama (as of 00000E7B)
- 大魔女様 -> Great Witch (as of 00000E7B; the OFFICE, see below)
- 女神ブルーシャ -> the Goddess Bruxa (as of 00000F24:106:92; her mummy, renamed. Portuguese "bruxa" = witch -> **Q378, owner-blocking**)

**新村エリカ — Erika Niimura** (にいむら エリカ; KATAKANA given name)
- エリカさん -> Erika-san (as of 00002026) · エリカちゃん -> Erika-chan (as of 00000A30)
- おばあさん -> Grandma (as of 0000095B) · おばあちゃん -> Grandma (as of 0000110D) · ばあちゃん -> Gran (as of 0000095B; keep the three distinct)
- お義母さん -> Mother (as of 0000095B; the in-law form) · かあさん / 母さん -> Mom (as of 00000B9F, 00000DB9)
- 大魔女様 -> Great Witch (as of 00000B27) · 長 -> the headman (00000A30:11:111) · 長老 -> the elder (00000A30:11:174; she says she prefers エリカ to it)
- コスプレババア -> the cosplay granny (as of 00001D17; 新村夏菜's and 新村サクラ's 3p for her, and 春花 uses the same word TO HER FACE at 0000095B:11:93)

**城崎健吾 — Kengo Shirozaki** (しろざき けんご per 人物名簿 — **but see §4.0**, CAST.md reads his wife's identical surname きざき/Kizaki)
- 城崎 (bare) -> Shirozaki · 城崎さん -> Shirozaki-san (as of 00001C12) · 城崎健吾さん -> Kengo Shirozaki-san (as of 00002444)
- 城崎のおっさん -> old man Shirozaki (as of 00001BCE)
- ケンちゃん -> Ken-chan (as of 0000243F)

### 4.2 Pre-modern and framing figures

**皇子 — the Prince** (おうじ; a title used as a name. No personal name anywhere in the project.)
- 皇子様 -> Your Highness / the Prince (as of 0000107B)
- **ニイ様 -> Nii-sama** (as of 00001505). Coined by 糸姫 at the founding, built out of 新村 and MISHEARD by him as 兄様 -> Q606. The EN must let one sound carry both readings, or take a TN.

**湊綱宗 — Minato no Tsunamune** (みなと の つなむね). Historical-style name with の; stays as is per STYLE.md.

**糸姫 — Ito-hime** (いとひめ)
- 糸 (bare) -> Ito. She is 糸 first; the 姫 is GRANTED ON SCREEN at 0000107B:11:73 as a kindness, not inherited -> **Q419 (owner-blocking)**. A mountain still carries the granted name 800 years later (糸姫山 -> Mount Itohime).
- 糸姫 -> Ito-hime · 糸姫ちゃん -> Ito-hime-chan · 糸ちゃん -> Ito-chan (as of 00002444) · 糸姫のお嬢ちゃん -> little Ito-hime
- お姫 -> the Ohime (as of 00001D17:12:0; the children's song's name for her) · 姫様 -> Hime-sama (as of 00001D6F:8:112; the same figure in a different register — en-collision noted in GLOSSARY)
- **LATER (as of 00001533): 後白河糸織** arrives with her face and no memory before the night she died; 糸織 is built on 糸. See §4.0 for the いおり/いとおり conflict.

**後白河糸織 — Iori Goshirakawa / Itoori Goshirakawa (reading UNRESOLVED, see §4.0)**
- 後白河さん -> Goshirakawa-san (as of 00001533) · 糸織 -> Iori/Itoori (bare, from 00001533:27:20)

**新村桔梗 — Kikyo Niimura** (にいむら ききょう)
- **Before her marriage she has NO SURNAME**: 「私の家系は商人なので、苗字はありません」 (00002137). Orders 366-375 she is 桔梗 only -> Kikyo.
- 桔梗 (bare) -> Kikyo · 桔梗殿 -> Kikyo-dono (as of 00002137; 新村晃's form for her UNTIL the marriage)
- 新村桔梗さん -> Kikyo Niimura-san (as of 000021A5) · 大魔女様 -> Great Witch
- 父上 -> Father (as of 0000212D; her address and reference form for her own father, who is unnamed)

**新村晃 — Ko Niimura / Akira Niimura (reading UNRESOLVED, see §4.0; -> Q595)**
- 晃様 -> Ko-sama (as of 00002137) -> **changes to** 晃さん -> Ko-san (as of 0000217A, after the marriage)
- Printed with ruby-residue spacing at 000014D7:8:165 -> Q595, Q010.

**藤吉郎 — Tokichiro** (とうきちろう)
- 吉郎 -> Kichiro (as of 00001092; 豊吉's form for him, and NOT a shortening the narration ever uses) · 藤殿 -> To-dono (as of 000010AA; 儀之助's form) -> Q443

**新村儀之助 — Ginosuke Niimura** (にいむら ぎのすけ). A Westerner using a Japanese name; the surname is the settlement's.
- 儀之助殿 -> Ginosuke-dono (as of 000010AA)
- **Original name: セレ -> Cele** (as of 000010DD:11:120; romanisation open -> **Q450, owner-blocking**)

**篠崎ハジメ — Hajime Shinozaki** (しのざき ハジメ; KATAKANA given name)
- 篠崎さん -> Shinozaki-san (as of 000021A5) · 篠崎君 -> Shinozaki-kun
- **RENAMED by his sect: 『金崎 勉』 -> Tsutomu Kanazaki** (as of 000021CF:8:69, printed with ruby-residue spacing -> Q877). From that point his superior calls him 金崎君 -> Kanazaki-kun.
- おじいちゃん -> Grandpa (from チャー坊) · この泥棒め -> you thief (as of 000021CF, from 新村桔梗's successor)

**ヴェルジ — Verde** (as of 00000F24:64:89; the 荒田 goddess. Glossed ON SCREEN at 100:149 as Portuguese for 緑色; katakana ヴェルジ = Brazilian "verde" -> **Q378, owner-blocking**)
- **Her living name: トミ -> Tomi** (00000F24:100:81, 「生前は『トミ』と呼ばれていた」)

**内府 — the Daifu** (だいふ; a court title, Minister of the Center, used as a name. TN at first occurrence.) **殿下 — His Highness** (でんか; the ruler of the realm, never given a personal name -> Q421).

### 4.3 Offices and collective names that read like personal names

- **大魔女 -> the Great Witch** (だいまじょ). An OFFICE, not a name, and it moves: 新村エリカ in the story present (as of 00000779:8:43, addressed 大魔女様); **LATER** 新村桔梗 two generations back (as of 00000AAC:11:16); 新村椿 -> Tsubaki Niimura in 祀耀482 (as of 000021BD:8:6, the 椿 of 00001355:8:83 with a surname, -> Q535); 新村晃's unnamed mother in 祀耀678 (CAST keeps her SEPARATE from the 00002026 先代大魔女); and in some branches the office passes to 新村サクラ and then to 新村茅萱 as 魔女 (-> Q109).
- **死神 -> the Shinigami / death god.** In 荒田 this is the settlement's ordinary word for a DEAD PERSON, not a figure (stated at 00000749). The masked attacker of 000004F9/0000050E/00000564 is also called 死神. Same word, two jobs -> Q052, Q213.
- **女神様 -> the Goddess** (as of 00000656:11:180). Named once with a 預言 attached (-> Q087); a second, higher deity is named at 00000F24 and never explained (-> Q386); a NEW 女神様 「荒田と元木の血を引いた」 supersedes 糸姫 at order 421 (-> Q978).
- **モトキザクラ様** (as of 000009FE). Has had its own CAST block since chunk 08; **LATER (as of 0000156A)** the legend is revealed to have been invented by an occult magazine to bury the 祀耀747 massacre (-> Q647, owner-blocking, Q648).
- **案内人 -> guide** (000015C2:81:2, her self-description). See 立木三日 under 新村春花.

### 4.4 Same EN, different people — do not merge

- **亀田 -> Kameda.** TWO people: the ハヤブサ宅配サービス call-centre clerk (000017D3:8:1) and the 株式会社IC receptionist at 古郡良治's employer (00002420). CAST keeps them as separate blocks and says so explicitly.
- **先代大魔女.** TWO women, ~120 years apart: 新村エリカ's mother-in-law at 00002026, and 新村晃's mother in 祀耀678. CAST keeps them separate.
- **新村椿 / 椿.** One person, two rows: 椿 named once at 00001355:8:83 with ruby-residue spacing, then 新村椿 with a surname at 000021BD:8:6 (-> Q535).
- **五島エリカ vs 新村エリカ.** 五島エリカ (00000989:11:33) is 五島絵梨奈's joke persona, one character off her own name. It is NOT 新村エリカ.

### 4.5 Names with a typographic hazard attached

- 南崎しのり -> Shinori Nanzaki. Printed 「南 崎」 with ruby-residue spacing at 00000EAF:40:144, 40:146, 46:96 -> Q268, Q010.
- 新村晃, 金崎勉, 椿 — all printed with ruby-residue spacing at their first occurrence -> Q595, Q877, Q535, Q010.
- 谷崎 -> Tanizaki. Text-only for 200 files and established FEMALE at chunk 25; her pupils' nickname 玲ちん -> Rei-chin is built on a given name beginning 玲 that never appears -> Q731.
- 向井 -> Mukai. Text-only (email) for 200 files; speaks for the first time at order 200 (00001533).
- ステラ優子 -> Yuko Stella. Katakana surname FIRST in the source, which Western order reverses; the oddity is the point.
- ケビン＝ホワイト -> Kevin White. The ＝ is a full-width double hyphen in the source; CP932 has it, but see STYLE.md's character set.
- 光源氏 and ルイス・キャロル (00000EDF:202:266-267) carry an ON-SCREEN parenthetical gloss 「（源氏物語の主人公）」 / 「（不思議の国のアリスの作者）」 — keep the gloss.
- A子 -> Girl A (000009FE:11:19). An anonymising device inside a told story, not a name -> Q159.
- 土田 -> Tsuchida (000021CF:8:185). Stated at 8:199 to be a 偽名 of the 教団本部長 -> Q878.
